# Archicad API coverage

Every function of the Archicad API (C functions, the C++ API and the Archicad 25/26 command ids), and whether the Tapir Add-On uses it. Generated by `tools/generate_api_coverage.py`; do not edit by hand.

**743 of 2414** applicable Archicad API items are used by Tapir (30 %): ✅ 743 done, 🚧 186 in progress, 🙋 172 claimed, ⬜ 1313 open, ➖ 469 not applicable.

| Part of the API | Used | Applicable | ✅ | 🔀 | 🚧 | 🙋 | ⬜ | ⛔ | ➖ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C functions | 34 % | 748 | 259 |  | 177 | 100 | 212 |  | 165 |
| C functions of Archicad 25/26 only, and their command ids | 21 % | 90 | 19 |  | 7 | 8 | 56 |  | 49 |
| C++ API (ACAPI::, IFCAPI::) | 29 % | 1576 | 465 |  | 2 | 64 | 1045 |  | 255 |

Archicad 25–30 DevKits; updated 2026-10-19 17:07 UTC.

| | Status |
|---|---|
| ✅ | used on `main` |
| 🔀 | used on `main`, not yet in a release |
| 🚧 | an open pull request adds it |
| 🙋 | an open issue names it: someone is on it, check there before starting |
| ⬜ | open: file an "Archicad API request" issue naming it to claim it |
| ⛔ | a maintainer decided not to expose it (reason given) |
| ➖ | not usable from a JSON command (reason given) |

Names are the newest ones; older names (renamed functions, AC25/26 command ids) are listed with them. "Used" means Tapir calls it somewhere, not that every option is exposed. C++ member functions count as used when a file that uses their class calls a function of that name.

## Element

- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_AddClassificationItem` – Classifies an element.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_AddClassificationItemDefault` – Classifies an element. — [#1140](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1140 "[2/3] Get/SetClassificationsOfElementDefaults commands"), [#944](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/944 "Get and set property values and classifications of tool defaults")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_AttachObserver` – Attaches an observer to the given element.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_CalcBounds` – Returns the model extent of an element in the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_CalcBoundsID`)</sub> — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings"), [#826](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/826 "GetDetailsOfElements: read the settings of a Stair")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Element_CalcCoreOffsetToSideOffset` – Converts the reveal depth offset from core skin to wall side for wall openings. <sub>(was `APIAny_CalcCoreOffsetToSideOffsetID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Element_CalcSideOffsetToCoreOffset` – Converts the reveal depth offset from wall side to core skin for wall openings. <sub>(was `APIAny_CalcSideOffsetToCoreOffsetID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_Element_CatchChangeDefaults` – Register or unregister your add-on to be notified if the default settings of a given element type is modified. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchChangeDefaults`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Element_CatchNewElement` – Register or unregister your add-on to be notified if the given type of element is created. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchNewElement`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Change` — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings"), [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#940](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/940 "Add ModifyCurtainWalls command"), [#918](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/918 "Add the ModifyOpenings command"), [#905](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/905 "ApplyFavoritesToElements: optional keepFields to keep the target's ID, size, sill, reference line and top link"), [#899](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/899 "Zones: expose height, base offset, top link and floor thickness; add ModifyZones"), [#843](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/843 "SetGDLParametersOfElements: say why Archicad refused the element"), [#834](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/834 "SetGDLParametersOfElements: reject the mirrored size parameters of curtain wall frames"), [#813](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/813 "Keep 2D GDL arrays intact on flat-list writes, apply Favorites to windows and doors, capture opening markers"), [#756](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/756 "Rename Project Map viewpoints through their element and verify renames"), [#463](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/463 "SetDetailsOfElements: modify any element type with the Modify commands' type-specific parameters"), [#1226](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1226 "Expose ACAPI_Element_ChangeMore (apply one masked change to many elements, like multi-select + settings)"), [#913](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/913 "Add ModifyCurtainWalls command"), [#662](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/662 "ModifySlabs: floorFill.orientation.origin appears read-only via API (type and matrix write correctly, origin does not)"), [#371](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/371 "Lessons learned building a Tapir-based automation toolkit (AC29)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Element_ChangeContent` – Change the content of a text or a textual label element. <sub>(was `APIAny_ChangeContentID`)</sub> — [#1095](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1095 "Add ChangeTextContent (ACAPI_Element_ChangeContent) and rawText of Text/Label content"), [#1092](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1092 "Expose ACAPI_Element_ChangeContent, and return the raw Autotext keys of a Text (edit one line without losing the Autotexts)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeDefaults` – Changes the default parameters of an element. — [#652](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/652 "Drawing favorite fails every time")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeDefaultsExt` – Changes the default parameters of the Window, Door, Section, Elevation, Interior Elevation, ChangeMarker, Detail, Worksheet, or Stair default element and its marker(s). This function also allows you to change the default settings of the Curtain Wall and its subelem.
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Element_ChangeElementInfoString` – Changes the compound info string of the given element. <sub>(was `APIDb_ChangeElementInfoStringID`)</sub> — [#371](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/371 "Lessons learned building a Tapir-based automation toolkit (AC29)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeExt` – Modifies a Window, Door, Section, Elevation, Interior Elevation, ChangeMarker, Detail, Worksheet, Stair or External element in the current database. — [#811](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/811 "Get/Set door & window marker settings, and Curtain Wall frame/panel class assignment")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeMemo` – Changes the memo part of certain element types. — [#906](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/906 "Make the Element ID settable through SetDetailsOfElements and the Create* commands"), [#905](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/905 "ApplyFavoritesToElements: optional keepFields to keep the target's ID, size, sill, reference line and top link"), [#902](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/902 "Element ID: settable through SetDetailsOfElements and the Create* commands (today read-only)")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Element_ChangeMore` – Modifies a number of elements in the current database. — [#1226](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1226 "Expose ACAPI_Element_ChangeMore (apply one masked change to many elements, like multi-select + settings)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeMorphEdgeType` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Modifies a morph element's edge type in the current database.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeParameters` – Changes the parameters of a number of elements. — [#905](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/905 "ApplyFavoritesToElements: optional keepFields to keep the target's ID, size, sill, reference line and top link"), [#813](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/813 "Keep 2D GDL arrays intact on flat-list writes, apply Favorites to windows and doors, capture opening markers")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Create` — [#1214](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1214 "Add SolveStairBaseline command (ACAPI_HierarchicalEditing_SolveStair)"), [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings"), [#827](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/827 "CreateZones refuses an overlapping zone instead of opening the modal warning (#822)"), [#750](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/750 "CreateStairs: pin an explicit treadDepth in the stair rules so it takes effect"), [#743](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/743 "CreateLabels: Symbol labels get their library part parameters (#742)"), [#742](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/742 "CreateLabels: Symbol labels are created without their GDL parameters"), [#578](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/578 "CreateStairs rejects multi-segment baselines that turn clockwise (-2130313215)"), [#567](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/567 "CreateAssociativeDimensionsOnSection still returns ghost GUIDs: the #512 guard was not applied to it (Interior Elevation, 1.5.8)"), [#531](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/531 "CreateWalls: a complex-profile Wall tool default silently overrides explicit parameters (defaults memo leak)"), [#425](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/425 "CreateStairs: stepNum / treadDepth / riserHeight are ignored — run length is fixed regardless of parameters")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_CreateExt` – Places a new Window, Door, Skylight, Section, Elevation, Interior Elevation, ChangeMarker, Detail, or Worksheet element with the specified marker into the current database. — [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#481](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/481 "CreateWindows/CreateDoors on AC29: command returns a GUID but no opening is created (single item, no crash) — even with a valid favoriteName")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Element_Decompose` – Enumerates the planes of a Multi-plane Roof.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Delete` – Deletes a number of elements. — [#906](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/906 "Make the Element ID settable through SetDetailsOfElements and the Create* commands"), [#904](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/904 "DeleteElements deletes the zones of a call before the other elements (#901)"), [#751](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/751 "DeleteElements: a guid that is not in the project no longer blocks the batch, and reports APIERR_BADID (#749)")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Element_DeleteSelected` – Deletes the selected elements.
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Element_DetachObserver` – Detaches the observer from the given element. — [#763](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/763 "Do not build the parent element in the element observer")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Element_DrawElement` – Shows/draws an existing element. <sub>(was `APIDb_DrawElementID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Edit` – Transforms a set of elements. — [#915](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/915 "Add the MirrorElements command and its Grasshopper component"), [#912](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/912 "Add ElevateElements command (vertical move)"), [#900](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/900 "UpdateZones: optional list of Zones to update instead of the current selection"), [#908](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/908 "Add ElevateElements command (vertical move)"), [#907](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/907 "Add MirrorElements command")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Elem2UIPriority` – Returns user interface priority for a building material connection priority. <sub>(was `APIAny_Elem2UIPriorityID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Element_ElemHasVisibleSkin` – Returns if elem has visible skin depending on Partial Structure Display. <sub>(was `APIAny_ElemHasVisibleSkinID`)</sub> — [#1133](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1133 "Add GetVisibleSkinStatus command (ACAPI_Element_ElemHasVisibleSkin)"), [#1124](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1124 "Expose ACAPI_Element_ElemHasVisibleSkin (is an element visible under a Partial Structure Display mode)")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Element_ElemTypeToNeigID` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns a neig for the given element type. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Goodies_ElemTypeToNeigID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Filter` – Tests an element by some given criteria. — [#1035](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1035 "[2/3] Store custom script data on elements: SetElementUserData, GetElementUserData"), [#917](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/917 "FilterElements: add layer, story and element type filters"), [#900](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/900 "UpdateZones: optional list of Zones to update instead of the current selection"), [#843](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/843 "SetGDLParametersOfElements: say why Archicad refused the element")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Get` – Returns information on the main parameters of a given element. — [#1167](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1167 "Add GetElementsInDrawOrder command exposing ACAPI_DrawOrder_DrawOrderGetNext"), [#1115](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1115 "Add GetHotlinkDesignOptionTables and SetHotlinkDesignOptionTables"), [#1095](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1095 "Add ChangeTextContent (ACAPI_Element_ChangeContent) and rawText of Text/Label content"), [#1086](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1086 "Add GetCalculatedParametersOfCurtainWallSubElements (ACAPI_CurtainWall_GetCWSubElemParams)"), [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#940](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/940 "Add ModifyCurtainWalls command"), [#918](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/918 "Add the ModifyOpenings command"), [#905](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/905 "ApplyFavoritesToElements: optional keepFields to keep the target's ID, size, sill, reference line and top link"), [#899](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/899 "Zones: expose height, base offset, top link and floor thickness; add ModifyZones"), [#827](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/827 "CreateZones refuses an overlapping zone instead of opening the modal warning (#822)"), [#813](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/813 "Keep 2D GDL arrays intact on flat-list writes, apply Favorites to windows and doors, capture opening markers"), [#756](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/756 "Rename Project Map viewpoints through their element and verify renames"), [#463](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/463 "SetDetailsOfElements: modify any element type with the Modify commands' type-specific parameters")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Element_GetActualOverrideStyle` – Get actual (merged) visual override style for an element. — [#942](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/942 "Graphic overrides: get and set the current window's override combination, read an element's effective override style")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetClassificationInSystem` – Retrieves a classification of an elem in a given classification system.

- ⬜ `ACAPI_Element_IsPropertyDefinitionVisibleDefault` – Tells whether a property definition is visible for an element default.

## Attribute

- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_AddClassificationItem` – Classifies an attribute. — [#1008](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1008 "Add GetClassificationsOfAttributes and SetClassificationsOfAttributes"), [#985](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/985 "Classifications of attributes: get, add and remove")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Create` — [#842](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/842 "Fix CreateZoneCategories failing with Failed to create. for every input"), [#781](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/781 "Full Get/Set/Create access to Model View Option combinations, including custom GDL-contributed fields")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_CreateAttributeFolderPicker` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Enables the attribute folder picker functionality for a push check. If the user clicks the push check, the picker will open. Attributes folder pickers can be used to choose an attribute folder.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_CreateExt`
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_CreateFolder` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Creates attribute folders. To create a folder, its full path has to be provided. The command will create all folders along the path, if they do not exist. — [#1024](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1024 "[2/4] Add RenameAttributeFolders and MoveAttributesAndFolders commands"), [#953](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/953 "[1/4] Add CreateAttributeFolders and DeleteAttributeFolders commands")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Delete` – Deletes an attribute.
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_DeleteFolder` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Deletes an attribute folder and all the folders and attributes it contains. — [#1024](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1024 "[2/4] Add RenameAttributeFolders and MoveAttributesAndFolders commands"), [#953](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/953 "[1/4] Add CreateAttributeFolders and DeleteAttributeFolders commands")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_DeleteMore` – Deletes more attributes of the same type.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_EnumerateAttributesByType` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Iterates the instances of a given attribute type in the database.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Get` – Retrieves an attribute from the database. — [#1221](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1221 "Add GetAttributesFromProject: read attributes from another project or template file"), [#1121](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1121 "Add GetCurrentLayerCombination command"), [#975](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/975 "Add ModifyPens: change single pens of the active pen table"), [#916](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/916 "Add ActivateLayerCombination command and Grasshopper component"), [#900](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/900 "UpdateZones: optional list of Zones to update instead of the current selection"), [#899](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/899 "Zones: expose height, base offset, top link and floor thickness; add ModifyZones"), [#843](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/843 "SetGDLParametersOfElements: say why Archicad refused the element"), [#842](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/842 "Fix CreateZoneCategories failing with Failed to create. for every input"), [#910](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/910 "Add command to activate a layer combination")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetAttributesByType` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the instances of a given attribute type in the database. — [#975](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/975 "Add ModifyPens: change single pens of the active pen table"), [#842](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/842 "Fix CreateZoneCategories failing with Failed to create. for every input")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_GetAttributesFromProject` – Loads the given attributes from a project file. — [#1221](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1221 "Add GetAttributesFromProject: read attributes from another project or template file"), [#1216](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1216 "Expose ACAPI_Attribute_GetAttributesFromProject (read/load attributes from another .pln/.tpl)")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetClassificationInSystem` – Retrieves a classification of an attribute in a given classification system.
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_GetClassificationItems` – Retrieves all of the classifications of an attribute. — [#1008](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1008 "Add GetClassificationsOfAttributes and SetClassificationsOfAttributes"), [#985](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/985 "Classifications of attributes: get, add and remove")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_GetCreationInfo` <sub>AC30</sub> – Retrieves creation metadata for an attribute. — [#997](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/997 "GetAttributesByType: report attribute creation info on Archicad 30"), [#948](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/948 "GetAttributesByType: add attribute creation info on Archicad 30 (method, source, creator, time)")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetCurrPenSet` – Get current pen set index. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetCurrPenSetID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetCurrentAttributeSetReader` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns an attribute reader for the currently used attribute set.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetDef` – Retrieves extended information for an attribute. — [#842](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/842 "Fix CreateZoneCategories failing with Failed to create. for every input")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetDefExt` – Retrieves extended information for an attribute. — [#975](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/975 "Add ModifyPens: change single pens of the active pen table")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetFirstValidAttribute` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the first valid attribute index.
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_GetFolder` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Retrieves an existing attribute folder by its path or guid. — [#1026](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1026 "[3/4] Add folderPath to GetAttributesByType"), [#1024](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1024 "[2/4] Add RenameAttributeFolders and MoveAttributesAndFolders commands"), [#953](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/953 "[1/4] Add CreateAttributeFolders and DeleteAttributeFolders commands")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_GetFolderContent` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns all folders and attributes of an attribute type. — [#1026](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1026 "[3/4] Add folderPath to GetAttributesByType")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetNum` – Returns the number of instances of a given type of attribute.
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPen` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns a pen by index from the active pen table. — [#951](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/951 "Add ModifyPens: change single pens of the active pen table")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPenNum` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Get the number of pens in the active pen table.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPropertyDefinitions` – Retrieves all of the built-in property definitions that are available for an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPropertyValue` – Retrieves the property value of the property definition.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPropertyValues` – Retrieves the property values of the property definitions.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPropertyValuesByGuid` – Retrieves the property values of the property definitions.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_IsClassificationItemVisible` – Tells whether a classification item is visible for an attribute.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_IsPropertyDefinitionAvailable` – Tells whether a property definition is available for an attribute.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_IsPropertyDefinitionValueEditable` – Tells whether a property definition is editable for an attribute.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_IsPropertyDefinitionVisible` – Tells whether a property definition is visible for an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Modify` – Modifies an attribute. — [#842](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/842 "Fix CreateZoneCategories failing with Failed to create. for every input")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_ModifyExt` – Modifies an attribute. — [#975](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/975 "Add ModifyPens: change single pens of the active pen table")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Attribute_ModifyPen` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Modify a pen by index in the active pen table. — [#951](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/951 "Add ModifyPens: change single pens of the active pen table")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_ModifyPropertyValue` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Sets the value of a property for multiple attributes. If the property is not available for at least one of the given attribute then returns with an error without changing any property value.
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_Move` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Moves attributes and attribute folders. — [#1024](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1024 "[2/4] Add RenameAttributeFolders and MoveAttributesAndFolders commands")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_RemoveClassificationItem` – Removes a classification from an attribute. — [#1008](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1008 "Add GetClassificationsOfAttributes and SetClassificationsOfAttributes"), [#985](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/985 "Classifications of attributes: get, add and remove")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Attribute_RenameFolder` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Deletes an attribute folder and all the folders and attributes it contains. — [#1024](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1024 "[2/4] Add RenameAttributeFolders and MoveAttributesAndFolders commands")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Search` – Searches for an attribute by name. — [#1066](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1066 "Add GetFontsByName command exposing ACAPI_Font_SearchFont")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_SetProperties` – Sets the specified built-in properties for the given values on an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_SetProperty` – Sets the property value of the property definition.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_AddProperty` <sub>AC27</sub> – Makes a property definition available for all of given attributes (it is created if it does not exist).
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_DeleteProperty` <sub>AC27</sub> – Makes a property definition unavailable for all of given attributes.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetUserData` <sub>AC25–26</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Attribute_SetUserData` <sub>AC25–26</sub>

## View

- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Change3DCuttingPlanes` – Changes the 3D cutting planes. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Change3DCuttingPlanesID`)</sub> — [#1139](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1139 "Set3DCutPlanes: switch the cut off with an empty array; add Get3DCutPlanes"), [#1046](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1046 "Set3DCutPlanes: allow switching cut planes off, add Get3DCutPlanes (split from #876)"), [#876](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/876 "Feature request: Get/Set view commands")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_View_Change3DImageSets` – Changes the 3D image item settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Change3DImageSetsID`)</sub> — [#876](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/876 "Feature request: Get/Set view commands")
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Change3DProjectionSets` – Changes the parameters of the 3D projection. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Change3DProjectionSetsID`)</sub> — [#876](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/876 "Feature request: Get/Set view commands")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_Change3DStyle` – Modifies the parameters of an existing 3D style <sub>(was `APIEnv_Change3DStyleID`)</sub> — [#1130](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1130 "Add Get3DStyleDetails, Create3DStyles and Change3DStyles commands"), [#1120](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1120 "Expose ACAPI_View_Get3DStyle / Create3DStyle / Change3DStyle (read, create and edit 3D styles) - complements #921")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_View_Change3DWindowSets` – Changes the 3D window settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Change3DWindowSetsID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_ChangeAutoIntersect` – Modifies the automatic wall intersection flag. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeAutoIntersectID`)</sub> — [#962](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/962 "[1/3] Add GetAutoIntersect and SetAutoIntersect commands"), [#935](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/935 "Get and set snap grid and auto-intersect settings")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_View_ChangeDocumentFrom3DDefaults` – Changes the 3D document setting default values. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeDocumentFrom3DDefaultsID`)</sub>
- 🙋 `ACAPI_View_ChangeDocumentFrom3DSettings` – Changes the 3D document settings of the specified database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeDocumentFrom3DSettingsID`)</sub> — [#1048](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1048 "Get/Set3DDocumentSettings: read and write the settings of a 3D Document (split from #876)"), [#876](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/876 "Feature request: Get/Set view commands")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_ChangeGhostRecord` – Change the settings of a Virtual Trace (ghost) record. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeGhostRecordID`)</sub> — [#972](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/972 "Add GetGhostStorySettings and SetGhostStorySettings commands"), [#936](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/936 "Get and set ghost story settings")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_ChangeShowHideState` – Changes the visibility of application. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeShowHideStateID`)</sub> — [#949](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/949 "Add HideElements and ShowAllHiddenElements commands")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_View_CoordToPoint` – Converts a model coordinate to screen coordinate in the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_CoordToPointID`)</sub> — [#1225](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1225 "Expose ACAPI_View_ModelToScreen / CoordToPoint / PointToCoord (model ↔ screen coordinates)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_Create3DStyle` – Creates a new 3D style. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Create3DStyleID`)</sub> — [#1130](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1130 "Add Get3DStyleDetails, Create3DStyles and Change3DStyles commands"), [#1120](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1120 "Expose ACAPI_View_Get3DStyle / Create3DStyle / Change3DStyle (read, create and edit 3D styles) - complements #921")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_CreateGhostRecord` – Create a new Trace (ghost) database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CreateGhostRecordID`)</sub> — [#972](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/972 "Add GetGhostStorySettings and SetGhostStorySettings commands")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_View_DeleteGhostRecord` – Deletes a Virtual Trace (ghost) record. <sub>(was `APIEnv_DeleteGhostRecordID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Get3DCuttingPlanes` – Retrieves the 3D cutting plane settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DCuttingPlanesID`)</sub> — [#1139](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1139 "Set3DCutPlanes: switch the cut off with an empty array; add Get3DCutPlanes"), [#876](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/876 "Feature request: Get/Set view commands")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_View_Get3DImageSets` – Returns the 3D image item settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DImageSetsID`)</sub> — [#876](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/876 "Feature request: Get/Set view commands")
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Get3DProjectionSets` – Returns information on the 3D projection settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DProjectionSetsID`)</sub> — [#876](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/876 "Feature request: Get/Set view commands")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_Get3DStyle` – Retrieves all parameters of a 3D style <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DStyleID`)</sub> — [#1130](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1130 "Add Get3DStyleDetails, Create3DStyles and Change3DStyles commands"), [#1120](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1120 "Expose ACAPI_View_Get3DStyle / Create3DStyle / Change3DStyle (read, create and edit 3D styles) - complements #921")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_Get3DStyleList` – Retrieves the names of all styles in the project and/or the name of the current style. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DStyleListID`)</sub> — [#954](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/954 "Add Get3DStyles, SetCurrent3DStyle and ShowElementsIn3D commands"), [#1120](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1120 "Expose ACAPI_View_Get3DStyle / Create3DStyle / Change3DStyle (read, create and edit 3D styles) - complements #921"), [#921](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/921 "Add 3D style commands and show selection / all in 3D")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_View_Get3DWindowSets` – Retrieves the 3D Window settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DWindowSetsID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_GetCurrentImage` <sub>AC30</sub> – Gets an image of the current view (3D, or rendering). — [#974](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/974 "Add the GetCurrentViewImage command: the 3D or rendering window image without a file (Archicad 30)"), [#950](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/950 "Add GetCurrentViewImage: return the 3D or rendering window image without a file (Archicad 30)")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_View_GetDocumentFrom3DDefaults` – Returns the 3D document setting default values. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetDocumentFrom3DDefaultsID`)</sub>
- 🙋 `ACAPI_View_GetDocumentFrom3DSettings` – Returns the 3D document settings of the specified database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetDocumentFrom3DSettingsID`)</sub> — [#1048](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1048 "Get/Set3DDocumentSettings: read and write the settings of a 3D Document (split from #876)"), [#876](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/876 "Feature request: Get/Set view commands")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_GetGhostRecord` – Retrieve a Trace record from the database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetGhostRecordID`)</sub> — [#972](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/972 "Add GetGhostStorySettings and SetGhostStorySettings commands"), [#936](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/936 "Get and set ghost story settings")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_GetGhostStorySettings` – Retrieves the current ghost story settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetGhostStorySettingsID`)</sub> — [#972](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/972 "Add GetGhostStorySettings and SetGhostStorySettings commands"), [#936](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/936 "Get and set ghost story settings")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_View_GetShowHideState` – Returns the visibility of application. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetShowHideStateID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_GetZoom` – Returns the actual zoom parameters of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetZoomID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_GoToView` – Switches to the given navigator view. <sub>(was `APIDo_GoToViewID`)</sub> — [#648](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/648 "Resync the source view's 2D transform before relinking a Drawing"), [#645](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/645 "ChangeDrawingLink produces a Drawing showing the wrong model content (position wrong, orientation right)")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_View_IsAutoGroupOn` – Returns the current state of the autogroup mode. <sub>(was `APIEnv_IsAutoGroupOnID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_IsAutoIntersectOn` – Returns the state of the automatic wall intersection flag. <sub>(was `APIEnv_IsAutoIntersectOnID`)</sub> — [#962](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/962 "[1/3] Add GetAutoIntersect and SetAutoIntersect commands"), [#935](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/935 "Get and set snap grid and auto-intersect settings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_IsSuspendGroupOn` – Returns the current state of the Suspend Groups mode. <sub>(was `APIEnv_IsSuspendGroupOnID`)</sub>
- 🙋 `ACAPI_View_ModelToScreen` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Projects a model coordinate to screen in the current view. — [#1225](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1225 "Expose ACAPI_View_ModelToScreen / CoordToPoint / PointToCoord (model ↔ screen coordinates)")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_View_PointToCoord` – Converts a screen coordinate into a model coordinate in the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_PointToCoordID`)</sub> — [#1225](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1225 "Expose ACAPI_View_ModelToScreen / CoordToPoint / PointToCoord (model ↔ screen coordinates)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Rebuild` – Rebuilds the content of the current window. <sub>(was `APIDo_RebuildID`)</sub> — [#984](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/984 "Add ZoomToElements (2D and 3D)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Redraw` – Redraws the content of the current window. <sub>(was `APIDo_RedrawID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_View_ResetZoom` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Restores the zoom parameters of the current database by a given number of steps.
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_SetCurrent3DStyle` – Sets an existing style as active. <sub>(was `APIEnv_SetCurrent3DStyleID`)</sub> — [#954](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/954 "Add Get3DStyles, SetCurrent3DStyle and ShowElementsIn3D commands"), [#1120](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1120 "Expose ACAPI_View_Get3DStyle / Create3DStyle / Change3DStyle (read, create and edit 3D styles) - complements #921"), [#921](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/921 "Add 3D style commands and show selection / all in 3D")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_View_SetZoom` – Sets the zoom box of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_SetZoomID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_ShowAllIn3D` – Activates the 3D window and shows the whole model. <sub>(was `APIDo_ShowAllIn3DID`, `APIIo_ShowAllIn3DID`)</sub> — [#954](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/954 "Add Get3DStyles, SetCurrent3DStyle and ShowElementsIn3D commands"), [#921](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/921 "Add 3D style commands and show selection / all in 3D")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_ShowSelectionIn3D` – Activates the 3D window and shows the selected elements. <sub>(was `APIDo_ShowSelectionIn3DID`, `APIIo_ShowSelectionIn3DID`)</sub> — [#954](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/954 "Add Get3DStyles, SetCurrent3DStyle and ShowElementsIn3D commands"), [#921](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/921 "Add 3D style commands and show selection / all in 3D")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_View_StoreViewSettings` – Stores or restores the current view settings. <sub>(was `APIDb_StoreViewSettingsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Zoom` – Performs a zoom operation. <sub>(was `APIDo_ZoomID`)</sub> — [#833](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/833 "Add ZoomToBox command to zoom the current 2D window to a model box")
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_ZoomToElements` – Zoom to the given elements. <sub>(was `APIDo_ZoomToElementsID`)</sub> — [#1001](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1001 "Add ZoomToElements to zoom the current 2D or 3D window to elements")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_View_ZoomToSelected` – Zooming to the actual selection. (Even in 3D.) <sub>(was `APIDo_ZoomToSelectedID`)</sub> — [#1001](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1001 "Add ZoomToElements to zoom the current 2D or 3D window to elements"), [#984](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/984 "Add ZoomToElements (2D and 3D)")

## LibraryPart

- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_AddSection` – Adds a new section to the Library Part under creation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_AddSection`)</sub> — [#957](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/957 "[1/2] Add CreateLibraryParts command: GDL objects from scripts and parameters")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_ChangeAParameter` – Changes a value in an opened Library Part parameter list. <sub>(was `APIAny_ChangeAParameterID`)</sub> — [#834](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/834 "SetGDLParametersOfElements: reject the mirrored size parameters of curtain wall frames")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_CheckLibPartSubtypeOf` – Checks whether a library part unique ID refers to a subtype of another. <sub>(was `APIAny_CheckLibPartSubtypeOfID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_CheckLibPartSubtypeOfbyMain` – Checks whether a library part unique ID refers to a subtype of another. <sub>(was `APIAny_CheckLibPartSubtypeOfbyMainID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_CloseParameters` – Closes the Library Part parameter list opened for editing. <sub>(was `APIAny_CloseParametersID`)</sub> — [#843](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/843 "SetGDLParametersOfElements: say why Archicad refused the element")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_CompareLibPartUnIds` – Compares two library part unique IDs. <sub>(was `APIAny_CompareLibPartUnIdsID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_Create` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Starts to create a new Library Part from scratch. — [#957](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/957 "[1/2] Add CreateLibraryParts command: GDL objects from scripts and parameters")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_EndSection` – Closes the last opened section of the Library Part under creation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_EndSection`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_Get` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the data for a registered library part. — [#957](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/957 "[1/2] Add CreateLibraryParts command: GDL objects from scripts and parameters")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetActParameters` – Returns the actual (edited) values of a Library Part parameter list opened to edit. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetActParametersID`)</sub> — [#843](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/843 "SetGDLParametersOfElements: say why Archicad refused the element")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetBuiltInLibpartUnId` – Retrieves the GUID of a built-in library part. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetBuiltInLibpartUnIdID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetDetails` – Returns the detailed parameters of a registered Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetDetails`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetElemLibPartUnId` – Returns the library part unique identifier of the GDL based element (object/lamp/window/door). <sub>(was `APIAny_GetElemLibPartUnIdID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetHotspots` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Retrieves the hotspots of a registered Library Part.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetLastValidEmbeddedLPName` – Retrieves the last valid embedded library location. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetLastValidEmbeddedLPNameID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetLibPartLockStatus` – Returns the current lock status of the given library part. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLibPartLockStatusID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetLibPartRefGuid` – This function is used to obtain the reference guid of a library part. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLibPartRefGuidID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetLibPartToolVariation` – Returns the library part's creator element type and variation ID. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetLibPartToolVariationID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetLibPartUnIDStr` – This function is used to obtain the given library part's own unique ID. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLibPartUnIDStrID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetMarkerParent` – Returns the parent ID of the marker, in the parentUnID member of the libPart structure. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Goodies_GetMarkerParent`, `APIAny_GetMarkerParentID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetNum` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the number of registered library parts. — [#957](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/957 "[1/2] Add CreateLibraryParts command: GDL objects from scripts and parameters")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetParamValues` – Returns the possible values defined for a Library Part parameter. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetParamValuesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetParams` – Returns the default parameters of a registered Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetParams`)</sub> — [#842](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/842 "Fix CreateZoneCategories failing with Failed to create. for every input"), [#743](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/743 "CreateLabels: Symbol labels get their library part parameters (#742)"), [#742](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/742 "CreateLabels: Symbol labels are created without their GDL parameters")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetSect_2DDrawHdl` – Returns the 2D binary (drawing) section data created from API elements. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetSect_2DDrawHdl`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetSect_ParamDef` – Prepares the parameter section data to save into a Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetSect_ParamDef`)</sub> — [#957](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/957 "[1/2] Add CreateLibraryParts command: GDL objects from scripts and parameters")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetSection` – Returns an existing section of a registered Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetSection`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetSectionList` – Returns the list of sections contained by a registered Library. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetSectionList`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetUnloadedLibpartName` – Retrieves the name of a Libpart, even if the library is unloaded. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetUnloadedLibpartNameID`)</sub> — [#1227](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1227 "Add GetMissingLibPartNames command (ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetUnloadedLibpartName)"), [#1218](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1218 "Expose ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetUnloadedLibpartName (name of a missing library part)")
- ➖ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_InstallESYMHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback for handling external symbols. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_NewSection` – Opens a new section to write into the Library Part under creation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_NewSection`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_OpenParameters` – Opens a Library Part parameter list for editing. <sub>(was `APIAny_OpenParametersID`)</sub> — [#843](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/843 "SetGDLParametersOfElements: say why Archicad refused the element"), [#743](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/743 "CreateLabels: Symbol labels get their library part parameters (#742)")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_PatternSearch` – Searches for registered Library Parts in the loaded libraries. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_PatternSearch`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_Register` – Registers a Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_Register`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_RegisterAll` – Registers an array of Library Parts. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_RegisterAll`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_RegisterESYM` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers an external symbol type with Archicad.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_RegisterSubtype` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers a new library part subtype.
- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_Save` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Saves the Library Part that has been created and registers it. — [#957](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/957 "[1/2] Add CreateLibraryParts command: GDL objects from scripts and parameters")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_Search` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Searches for a registered Library Part in the loaded libraries. — [#957](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/957 "[1/2] Add CreateLibraryParts command: GDL objects from scripts and parameters"), [#842](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/842 "Fix CreateZoneCategories failing with Failed to create. for every input")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_SetDetails_ParamDef` – Sets the detail parameters of a Library Part parameter section handle. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_SetDetails_ParamDef`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_SetUpSect_2DDrawHdl` – Sets up an empty 2D binary (drawing) section to fill up from API elements. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_SetUpSect_2DDrawHdl`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_ShapePrims` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Virtually draws the shape of a registered Library Part.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_UpdateSection` – Updates a section of a registered Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_UpdateSection`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_WriteSection` – Writes data into an opened section of the Library Part under creation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_WriteSection`)</sub>

## Navigator

- 🚧 `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeCurrLayerComb` – Activates a layer combination. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeCurrLayerCombID`)</sub> — [#916](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/916 "Add ActivateLayerCombination command and Grasshopper component")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeLayoutSets` – Changes the settings of the current layout in Archicad. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeLayoutSetsID`)</sub> — [#885](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/885 "RenameNavigatorItem: apply newId to View Map items and fail when the ID is not applied"), [#765](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/765 "RenameNavigatorItem: set a view's ID with newId and fail when the ID is kept"), [#884](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/884 "Renaming / re-IDing Project Map items, Schedules and Layout Book folders via RenameNavigatorItem"), [#764](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/764 "newId never sets a view's ID: RenameNavigatorItem writes it for layouts only, and still reports success")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeNavigatorItem` – Modifies the given Project Navigator item. <sub>(was `APINavigator_ChangeNavigatorItemID`)</sub> — [#885](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/885 "RenameNavigatorItem: apply newId to View Map items and fail when the ID is not applied"), [#756](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/756 "Rename Project Map viewpoints through their element and verify renames"), [#884](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/884 "Renaming / re-IDing Project Map items, Schedules and Layout Book folders via RenameNavigatorItem"), [#764](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/764 "newId never sets a view's ID: RenameNavigatorItem writes it for layouts only, and still reports success"), [#755](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/755 "RenameNavigatorItem reports success without renaming Project Map exterior elevations (AC28, Tapir 1.6.0); View Map control works")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeNavigatorVPItem` – Changes an existing Navigator viewpoint root, group or leaf node in the Project Map. <sub>(was `APINavigator_ChangeNavigatorVPItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeNavigatorView` – Sets the given Project Navigator item and its view settings. <sub>(was `APINavigator_ChangeNavigatorViewID`)</sub> — [#1047](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1047 "GetViewSettings: return the stored in this view flags and the full transformation (split from #876)"), [#688](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/688 "SetViewSettings fails at ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeNavigatorView (-2130313113) even when writing a view's current values")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeStructureDisplay` – Changes the partial structure display options for the current database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeStructureDisplayID`)</sub> — [#1102](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1102 "Add GetStructureDisplay / SetStructureDisplay (Partial Structure Display of the current window)"), [#1099](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1099 "Expose ACAPI_Navigator_GetStructureDisplay / ChangeStructureDisplay (Partial Structure Display of the current window)")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeViewOptions` – Changes the actual model view options. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeViewOptionsID`)</sub> — [#781](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/781 "Full Get/Set/Create access to Model View Option combinations, including custom GDL-contributed fields")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_CloneProjectMapItemToViewMap` – Clones a Navigator item from the project map to view map. <sub>(was `APINavigator_CloneProjectMapItemToViewMapID`)</sub> — [#876](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/876 "Feature request: Get/Set view commands")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_CreateLayout` – Creates a new layout. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CreateLayoutID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Navigator_CreateNavigatorVPItem` – Creates a new Navigator viewpoint root, group or leaf node in the Project Map. <sub>(was `APINavigator_CreateNavigatorVPItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_CreateSubSet` – Creates the Subset in the Layout Book. <sub>(was `APIDb_CreateSubSetID`)</sub> — [#1105](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1105 "Expose ACAPI_Navigator_GetSubSet (read the settings of an existing Layout Subset)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Navigator_DeleteNavigatorVPItem` – Deletes a Navigator viewpoint root, group or leaf node from the Project Map. <sub>(was `APINavigator_DeleteNavigatorVPItemID`)</sub> — [#691](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/691 "Fix DeleteNavigatorItems for Project Map viewpoints (schedules)"), [#1039](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1039 "DeleteNavigatorItems: delete independent Worksheets and Details through ACAPI_Database_DeleteDatabase (today Failed to delete navigator item.)"), [#690](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/690 "DeleteNavigatorItems fails on Project Map viewpoints (schedules): uses DeleteNavigatorView, which only handles View Map items")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_DeleteNavigatorView` – Deletes the given navigator view. <sub>(was `APINavigator_DeleteNavigatorViewID`)</sub> — [#1050](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1050 "DeleteNavigatorItems: delete independent worksheets and details of the Project Map"), [#1039](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1039 "DeleteNavigatorItems: delete independent Worksheets and Details through ACAPI_Database_DeleteDatabase (today Failed to delete navigator item.)"), [#690](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/690 "DeleteNavigatorItems fails on Project Map viewpoints (schedules): uses DeleteNavigatorView, which only handles View Map items")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Navigator_DimStand_Get` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Get a dimension preference by index or name. — [#1209](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1209 "Add GetDimensionStandards command (read dimension standards)"), [#1206](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1206 "Expose ACAPI_Navigator_DimStand_* (read dimension standards)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Navigator_DimStand_GetNum` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Get the number of dimension preferences. — [#1209](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1209 "Add GetDimensionStandards command (read dimension standards)"), [#1206](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1206 "Expose ACAPI_Navigator_DimStand_* (read dimension standards)")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Navigator_DimStand_Search` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Search a dimension preference by name or index. — [#1206](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1206 "Expose ACAPI_Navigator_DimStand_* (read dimension standards)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Navigator_GetCurrLayerComb` – Retrieves the index of the current layer combination. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetCurrLayerCombID`)</sub> — [#1121](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1121 "Add GetCurrentLayerCombination command"), [#1116](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1116 "Expose ACAPI_Navigator_GetCurrLayerComb (which layer combination the current window uses)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetLayoutBook` – Retrieves the Book Settings. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetLayoutBookID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetLayoutSets` – Returns the settings of a layout. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLayoutSetsID`)</sub> — [#885](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/885 "RenameNavigatorItem: apply newId to View Map items and fail when the ID is not applied"), [#825](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/825 "Change and report the master layout in SetLayoutSettings and GetLayoutSettings"), [#765](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/765 "RenameNavigatorItem: set a view's ID with newId and fail when the ID is kept"), [#884](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/884 "Renaming / re-IDing Project Map items, Schedules and Layout Book folders via RenameNavigatorItem"), [#764](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/764 "newId never sets a view's ID: RenameNavigatorItem writes it for layouts only, and still reports success")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorChildNum` – Retrieves the number of children of a navigator item. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorChildNumID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorChildrenItems` – Retrieves the child items of a navigator item. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorChildrenItemsID`)</sub> — [#761](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/761 "CreateLayout: let the caller choose the new layout's place in its subset")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorItem` – Retrieves a navigator item. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorItemID`)</sub> — [#1112](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1112 "Add GetLayoutSubsetSettings: read the settings of existing Layout Book subsets"), [#841](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/841 "DeleteNavigatorItems: report a refused delete as a failure instead of success"), [#825](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/825 "Change and report the master layout in SetLayoutSettings and GetLayoutSettings"), [#765](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/765 "RenameNavigatorItem: set a view's ID with newId and fail when the ID is kept"), [#761](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/761 "CreateLayout: let the caller choose the new layout's place in its subset"), [#756](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/756 "Rename Project Map viewpoints through their element and verify renames"), [#755](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/755 "RenameNavigatorItem reports success without renaming Project Map exterior elevations (AC28, Tapir 1.6.0); View Map control works")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorParentItem` – Retrieves the parent item of a navigator item. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorParentItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorSet` – Retrieves information about a Project Navigator Map or Publisher Set. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorSetID`)</sub> — [#761](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/761 "CreateLayout: let the caller choose the new layout's place in its subset")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorSetNum` – Retrieves the number of Publisher Sets. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorSetNumID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorVPItem` – Gets information about an existing Navigator viewpoint root, group or leaf node in the Project Map. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorVPItemID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorVPItemChildren` – Gets the list of child viewpoint groups/nodes of a Navigator viewpoint root or group. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorVPItemChildrenID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorVPRootGroups` – Gets the list of Navigator viewpoint roots. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorVPRootGroupsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorView` – Retrieves a navigator view. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorViewID`)</sub> — [#876](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/876 "Feature request: Get/Set view commands")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Navigator_GetStructureDisplay` – Changes the partial structure display options for the current database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetStructureDisplayID`)</sub> — [#1102](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1102 "Add GetStructureDisplay / SetStructureDisplay (Partial Structure Display of the current window)"), [#1099](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1099 "Expose ACAPI_Navigator_GetStructureDisplay / ChangeStructureDisplay (Partial Structure Display of the current window)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Navigator_GetSubSet` – Retrieves the Subset settings of the given Layout Subset. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetSubSetID`)</sub> — [#1112](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1112 "Add GetLayoutSubsetSettings: read the settings of existing Layout Book subsets"), [#1105](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1105 "Expose ACAPI_Navigator_GetSubSet (read the settings of an existing Layout Subset)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetSubSetDefault` – Retrieves the default Subset settings of the Layout Book. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetSubSetDefaultID`)</sub> — [#1105](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1105 "Expose ACAPI_Navigator_GetSubSet (read the settings of an existing Layout Subset)")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Navigator_GetViewOptions` – Returns the actual view display options. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetViewOptionsID`)</sub> — [#781](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/781 "Full Get/Set/Create access to Model View Option combinations, including custom GDL-contributed fields")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ModelViewOptions_Get` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Get an API_ModelViewOptionsType by index.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ModelViewOptions_GetNum` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Get the number of API_ModelViewOptions.
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Navigator_ModelViewOptions_Search` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Search an API_ModelViewOptions_Head by name or index. — [#781](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/781 "Full Get/Set/Create access to Model View Option combinations, including custom GDL-contributed fields")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_NewNavigatorView` – Creates a new Navigator View. <sub>(was `APINavigator_NewNavigatorViewID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Navigator_RegisterCallbackInterface` – Registers an object with virtual functions that are implemented by the AddOn. These are called by Archicad when certain events happen relating to the navigator items that are registered by the AddOn. <sub>(was `APINavigator_RegisterCallbackInterfaceID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_SearchNavigatorItem` – Searches the navigator items of the requested database. <sub>(was `APINavigator_SearchNavigatorItemID`)</sub> — [#1050](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1050 "DeleteNavigatorItems: delete independent worksheets and details of the Project Map"), [#825](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/825 "Change and report the master layout in SetLayoutSettings and GetLayoutSettings"), [#761](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/761 "CreateLayout: let the caller choose the new layout's place in its subset")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Navigator_SetMasterLayoutOnLayout` – Sets the layout context of current database for drawing. <sub>(was `APIAny_SetMasterLayoutOnLayoutID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_SetNavigatorItemPosition` – With this function a navigator item can be moved to another place. It can be used with items on the layout map and on the view maps (in case of teamworking on the public and on the users' own view map as well. <sub>(was `APINavigator_SetNavigatorItemPositionID`)</sub> — [#761](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/761 "CreateLayout: let the caller choose the new layout's place in its subset")

## General

- ⬜ `ACAPI_ActivatePropertyObjectHandler` – Activates or deactivates an add-on's property handler.
- ➖ `ACAPI_CallCommand` – Performs an operation without opening an undoable session. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_CallUndoableCommand` – Performs an undoable operation. — [#1204](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1204 "[1/6] Analytical commands: current analytical model, update, generation settings, model check type"), [#1188](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1188 "Add CreateElementSets command (ACAPI_ElementSet_Create)"), "Add SetGraphicOverrideRuleGroupOrder command"), [#1176](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1176 "Add RenameGraphicOverrideRuleGroups (ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_ChangeOverrideRuleGroup)"), [#1149](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1149 "[4/4] Make property definitions available or unavailable for elements - ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeAttrDefsHdlsExt` – Frees the memory occupied by all of the attribute definition handles. — [#1221](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1221 "Add GetAttributesFromProject: read attributes from another project or template file"), [#975](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/975 "Add ModifyPens: change single pens of the active pen table") *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeBeamRelationHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all allocated beam relation handles. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeBeamSegmentRelationHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all the allocated beam segment relation handles. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeElemMemoHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all of the allocated memo handles. — [#1214](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1214 "Add SolveStairBaseline command (ACAPI_HierarchicalEditing_SolveStair)"), [#1095](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1095 "Add ChangeTextContent (ACAPI_Element_ChangeContent) and rawText of Text/Label content"), [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#940](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/940 "Add ModifyCurtainWalls command"), [#906](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/906 "Make the Element ID settable through SetDetailsOfElements and the Create* commands"), [#905](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/905 "ApplyFavoritesToElements: optional keepFields to keep the target's ID, size, sill, reference line and top link"), [#826](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/826 "GetDetailsOfElements: read the settings of a Stair"), [#813](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/813 "Keep 2D GDL arrays intact on flat-list writes, apply Favorites to windows and doors, capture opening markers"), [#756](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/756 "Rename Project Map viewpoints through their element and verify renames"), [#463](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/463 "SetDetailsOfElements: modify any element type with the Modify commands' type-specific parameters") *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeParagraphsHdl` – Frees the memory occupied by a handle to an array of @ref API_ParagraphType. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeRoomRelationHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all allocated room relation handles. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeWallRelationHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all allocated wall relation handles. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_FreeCWContourPtr` – Frees the curtain wall contour pointer. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_FreeGDLModelViewOptionsPtr` – Frees the GDL model view options pointer. — [#781](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/781 "Full Get/Set/Create access to Model View Option combinations, including custom GDL-contributed fields") *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetExtensionInstance` – Returns the HINSTANCE of the add-on's DLL. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetMainWindow` – Returns the application's main window where the add-on works. *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetOwnLocation` – Returns the location of the add-on in the file system. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetOwnResModule` – Returns the add-on's resource module identifier for loading resources. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetPreferences` – Returns the stored preferences of the add-on. *(add-on preferences)*
- ⬜ `ACAPI_GetReleaseNumber`
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetToken` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the token associated with the AddOn. — [#1204](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1204 "[1/6] Analytical commands: current analytical model, update, generation settings, model check type"), [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings") *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_KeepInMemory` *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_RefreshTeamworkReserveInterface` – Refreshes the registered teamwork interface. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_RegisterModelessWindow` – Registers a modeless palette in the API. *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_RegisterTeamworkReserveInterface` – Registers an interface supporting reserve/release in teamwork operations. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_SetDllTermHookProc` – Sets a handler procedure for the case of abnormal termination. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_SetPreferences` – Saves custom preferences data into the preferences file. *(add-on preferences)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UnregisterModelessWindow` – Ends the registration of an add-on's modeless palette with the API. *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UnregisterTeamworkReserveInterface` – Unregisters a previously registered interface. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_WriteReport` – Writes a report string into the Report Window or into a custom alert window. *(report window of the add-on)*

## AddOnIntegration

- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallClassificationVisibilityHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the classification visibility handler function. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallElemSetSaveOldFormatHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for handling the conversion of the user data, the add-on attached to an element set. *(add-on registration and menus)*

- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallFileTypeHandler3D` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for handling file operations. *(add-on registration and menus)*

- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallNavigatorAddOnViewPointDataSaveOldFormatHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for converting Navigator AddOn viewpoints into an older format. *(add-on registration and menus)*

- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPublisherSaveTranslator` <sub>AC30</sub> – Installs the translator methods for the Publisher. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPublisherSaveWithOptionMethod` <sub>AC30</sub> – Installs the save methods for the Publisher with the set options. *(add-on registration and menus)*

- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_RegisterModulDataHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers the add-on to be called for merging and converting its module data. *(add-on registration and menus)*
