## Database

- ⬜ `ACAPI_Database_BuildWindowValidator` – Registers a new window validator for the given guid. <sub>(was `APIDb_BuildWindowValidatorID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_ChangeCurrentDatabase` – Changes the current (active) database. <sub>(was `APIDb_ChangeCurrentDatabaseID`)</sub> — [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#972](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/972 "Add GetGhostStorySettings and SetGhostStorySettings commands")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Database_CheckWindowValidator` – Cheks the already added list of elements and checksums form the validator (identified by the guid) against the lists from the parameter if there was any changes. <sub>(was `APIDb_CheckWindowValidatorID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_DeleteDatabase` – Deletes an independent detail, worksheet, layout, or master layout database. <sub>(was `APIDb_DeleteDatabaseID`)</sub> — [#1050](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1050 "DeleteNavigatorItems: delete independent worksheets and details of the Project Map"), [#1039](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1039 "DeleteNavigatorItems: delete independent Worksheets and Details through ACAPI_Database_DeleteDatabase (today Failed to delete navigator item.)"), [#690](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/690 "DeleteNavigatorItems fails on Project Map viewpoints (schedules): uses DeleteNavigatorView, which only handles View Map items")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Database_DoClip` – Clips the elements created during a clipping session. <sub>(was `APIDb_DoClipID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Database_GetContainingDatabase` – Obtains the unique ID of database contains the given element. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetContainingDatabaseID`)</sub> — [#1107](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1107 "Add GetContainingDatabases command to find an element's database"), [#1103](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1103 "Expose ACAPI_Database_GetContainingDatabase and ACAPI_Element_GetElementFromAnywhere (find and read an element without switching windows)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetCurrentDatabase` – Returns the identifier of the current (active) database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetCurrentDatabaseID`)</sub> — [#972](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/972 "Add GetGhostStorySettings and SetGhostStorySettings commands"), [#900](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/900 "UpdateZones: optional list of Zones to update instead of the current selection"), [#833](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/833 "Add ZoomToBox command to zoom the current 2D window to a model box")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Database_GetDBUnIdFromGuid` – Returns the old database identifiers from the database GUID. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetDBUnIdFromGuidID`)</sub>
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForType` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Helper function to collect all Archicad databases of the given type. — [#1128](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1128 "Expose ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes (list all sections, elevations, details, worksheets, 3D documents)")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Helper function to collect all Archicad databases of the given types. — [#1128](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1128 "Expose ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes (list all sections, elevations, details, worksheets, 3D documents)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Database_GetDetailDatabases` – Returns the list of unique IDs of all detail databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetDetailDatabasesID`)</sub> — [#1135](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1135 "Add GetDatabases command to list all databases of given types"), [#1128](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1128 "Expose ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes (list all sections, elevations, details, worksheets, 3D documents)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Database_GetDocumentFrom3DDatabases` – Returns the list of unique IDs of all document 3D databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetDocumentFrom3DDatabasesID`)</sub> — [#1135](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1135 "Add GetDatabases command to list all databases of given types"), [#1128](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1128 "Expose ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes (list all sections, elevations, details, worksheets, 3D documents)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Database_GetElevationDatabases` – Returns all the elevation databases in the project. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetElevationDatabasesID`)</sub> — [#1135](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1135 "Add GetDatabases command to list all databases of given types"), [#1128](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1128 "Expose ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes (list all sections, elevations, details, worksheets, 3D documents)")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Database_GetGuidFromDBUnId` – Returns the GUID from old database identifiers. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetGuidFromDBUnIdID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Database_GetInteriorElevationDatabases` – Returns all the interior elevation databases in the project. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetInteriorElevationDatabasesID`)</sub> — [#1135](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1135 "Add GetDatabases command to list all databases of given types"), [#1128](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1128 "Expose ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes (list all sections, elevations, details, worksheets, 3D documents)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetLayoutDatabases` – Returns the list of unique IDs of all layout databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetLayoutDatabasesID`)</sub> — [#1135](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1135 "Add GetDatabases command to list all databases of given types"), [#1128](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1128 "Expose ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes (list all sections, elevations, details, worksheets, 3D documents)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Database_GetLocOrigo` – Returns the local origin of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetLocOrigoID`)</sub> — [#1123](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1123 "Add GetCoordinateOrigins command (virtual origin offset and user origin)"), [#1117](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1117 "Expose ACAPI_ProjectSetting_GetOffset and ACAPI_Database_GetLocOrigo (virtual origin offset and user origin)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetMasterLayoutDatabases` – Returns the list of unique IDs of all master layout databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetMasterLayoutDatabasesID`)</sub> — [#1135](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1135 "Add GetDatabases command to list all databases of given types")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Database_GetSectionDatabases` – Returns all the section databases in the project. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetSectionDatabasesID`)</sub> — [#1135](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1135 "Add GetDatabases command to list all databases of given types"), [#1128](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1128 "Expose ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes (list all sections, elevations, details, worksheets, 3D documents)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Database_GetWorksheetDatabases` – Returns the list of unique IDs of all detail databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetWorksheetDatabasesID`)</sub> — [#1135](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1135 "Add GetDatabases command to list all databases of given types"), [#1128](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1128 "Expose ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes (list all sections, elevations, details, worksheets, 3D documents)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Database_JsonQuery` – Helper function to call various functions inside Archicad. <sub>(was `APIDb_JsonQueryID`)</sub> — [#1009](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1009 "Add ConsolidateLines, ConsolidateFills and GetUsedAttributes commands"), [#988](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/988 "Line and fill consolidation, and the list of used attributes")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Database_ModifyDatabase` – Modifies the parameters of an independent detail, worksheet, layout or master layout database. <sub>(was `APIDb_ModifyDatabaseID`)</sub> — [#825](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/825 "Change and report the master layout in SetLayoutSettings and GetLayoutSettings"), [#755](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/755 "RenameNavigatorItem reports success without renaming Project Map exterior elevations (AC28, Tapir 1.6.0); View Map control works")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_NewDatabase` – Creates a new independent detail, worksheet, layout or master layout database. <sub>(was `APIDb_NewDatabaseID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Database_RebuildCurrentDatabase` – Rebuilds the current database, even it's associated window is in the background. <sub>(was `APIDb_RebuildCurrentDatabaseID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Database_RebuildWindowValidator` – Sets the element and checksum list for an already added validator with given guid. <sub>(was `APIDb_RebuildWindowValidatorID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Database_RedrawCurrentDatabase` – Redraws the current database, even it's associated window is in the background. <sub>(was `APIDb_RedrawCurrentDatabaseID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Database_StartClippingSession` – Sets up a clipping session for newly created elements to be clipped. <sub>(was `APIDb_StartClippingSessionID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Database_StopClippingSession` – Stops clipping elements. <sub>(was `APIDb_StopClippingSessionID`)</sub>

## Teamwork

- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_FindLockableObjectSet` – Retrieves the unique identifier of a lockable object set with a string identifier. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_FindLockableObjectSet`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetHotlinkCacheManagementOwner` – Provides the current owner of the Hotlink/XRef Management in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_GetHotlinkCacheManagementOwner`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetLockableStatus` – Retrieves the reservation status of a lockable object set. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_GetLockableStatus`)</sub> — [#1021](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1021 "[2/3] Add GetReservationStatusOfElements and GrantElements Teamwork commands")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetTWAccessRight` – Returns if the current user has the specified access right. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetTWAccessRightID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetTWOwner` – Retrieves the TeamWork owner of a given database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetTWOwnerID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetTeamworkProjectDetails` – Extracts details from the given location about the teamwork project. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_GetTeamworkProjectDetails`)</sub> — [#955](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/955 "[1/3] Teamwork: add the GetTeamworkProjectInfo command"), [#791](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/791 "Add a way to open a Teamwork/BIMcloud project, not just local .pln/.pla files")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetUsernameFromId` – Retrieves the username of a user identified by a short id in a Teamwork project. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_GetUsernameFromId`)</sub> — [#1021](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1021 "[2/3] Add GetReservationStatusOfElements and GrantElements Teamwork commands"), [#955](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/955 "[1/3] Teamwork: add the GetTeamworkProjectInfo command")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Teamwork_GrantElements` – In teamwork mode this function grants the specified elements to the given user. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GrantElements`)</sub> — [#1021](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1021 "[2/3] Add GetReservationStatusOfElements and GrantElements Teamwork commands")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_HasConnection` – Tells whether the currently opened project is a Teamwork project. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_HasConnection`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_HasCreateRight` – Checks whether the current Teamwork user has access right to create a certain type of object. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_HasCreateRight`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_HasDeleteModifyRight` – Checks whether the current Teamwork user has access right to delete or modify a certain type of object. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_HasDeleteModifyRight`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Teamwork_IsOnline` – Tells whether the client is online connected to the BIM server. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_IsOnline`)</sub> — [#955](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/955 "[1/3] Teamwork: add the GetTeamworkProjectInfo command")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_IsServerLibPart` – Tells whether the given location refers to a BIM Server Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_IsServerLibPart`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ProjectSharing` – Returns the project sharing data in the case of a TeamWork project. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ProjectSharingID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReceiveChanges` – Receives changes from the BIMcloud server. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReceiveChanges`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReleaseElements` – Releases elements in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReleaseElements`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReleaseHotlinkCacheManagement` – Releases the Hotlink/XRef Management in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReleaseHotlinkCacheManagement`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReleaseLockable` – Releases a lockable object set in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReleaseLockable`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_RemoveLibraries` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Remove libraries from the BIMcloud server.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReserveElements` – Reserves elements in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReserveElements`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReserveHotlinkCacheManagement` – Reserves the Hotlink/XRef Management in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReserveHotlinkCacheManagement`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReserveLockable` – Reserves a lockable object set in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReserveLockable`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_SendChanges` – Sends changes to the BIMcloud server. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_SendChanges`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_SendReleaseCommentMail` – Sends the release comment mail. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkReserveInterface_SendReleaseCommentMail`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_UploadLibraries` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Uploads the given libraries to the BIMcloud server.

## GraphicalOverride

- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_ChangeOverrideCombination` – Changes an override combination identified by its guid. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Override_ChangeOverrideCombination`)</sub> — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_ChangeOverrideRule` – Changes an override rule identified by its guid. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Override_ChangeOverrideRule`)</sub> — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings"), [#561](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/561 "Wish: expose ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_* (create/modify/delete override rules and combinations)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_ChangeOverrideRuleGroup` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Changes an override rule group identified by its guid. — [#1176](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1176 "Add RenameGraphicOverrideRuleGroups (ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_ChangeOverrideRuleGroup)"), [#1162](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1162 "Expose ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_ChangeOverrideRuleGroup")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_CreateOverrideCombination` – Creates an override combination. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Override_CreateOverrideCombination`)</sub> — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_CreateOverrideRule` – Creates an override rule. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Override_CreateOverrideRule`)</sub> — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings"), [#561](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/561 "Wish: expose ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_* (create/modify/delete override rules and combinations)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_CreateOverrideRuleGroup` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Creates an override rule group. — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_DeleteOverrideCombination` – Deletes an override combination identified by its guid. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Override_DeleteOverrideCombination`)</sub> — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_DeleteOverrideRule` – Deletes an override rule identified by its guid. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Override_DeleteOverrideRule`)</sub> — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings"), [#561](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/561 "Wish: expose ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_* (create/modify/delete override rules and combinations)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_DeleteOverrideRuleGroup` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Deletes an override rule group identified by its guid and deletes all the override rules it contains. — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_EnableRenovationOverride` – Enables renovation graphical overrides during @ref ACAPI_DrawingPrimitive_ShapePrims. <sub>(was `APIAny_EnableRenovationOverrideID`)</sub> — [#1163](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1163 "Expose ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_EnableRenovationOverride")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_EnableVisualOverride` – Enables graphical overrides during @ref ACAPI_DrawingPrimitive_ShapePrims. <sub>(was `APIAny_EnableVisualOverrideID`)</sub> — [#1164](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1164 "Expose ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_EnableVisualOverride")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetActualOverrideCombination` <sub>AC30</sub> – Retrieves the override combination currently applied to the active window. — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings"), [#942](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/942 "Graphic overrides: get and set the current window's override combination, read an element's effective override style")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideCombination` – Retrieves an override combination identified by its guid or name. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Override_GetOverrideCombination`)</sub> — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideCombinationList` – Retrieves a list of all override combinations in the current plan. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Override_GetOverrideCombinationList`)</sub> — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideRuleById` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Retrieves an override rule identified by its guid. — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideRuleByName` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Retrieves an override rule identified by its name. — [#1068](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1068 "Add GetGraphicOverrideRulesByName (ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideRuleByName)"), [#1063](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1063 "Expose ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideRuleByName")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideRuleGroup` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Retrieves an override rule group identified by its guid or name. — [#1178](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1178 "Add SetGraphicOverrideRuleGroupOrder command"), [#1176](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1176 "Add RenameGraphicOverrideRuleGroups (ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_ChangeOverrideRuleGroup)"), [#1075](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1075 "Add SetGraphicOverrideRuleOrder: order the rules of a graphic override rule group"), [#1068](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1068 "Add GetGraphicOverrideRulesByName (ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideRuleByName)"), [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideRuleGroupList` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Retrieves a list of all override rule groups in the current plan. — [#1178](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1178 "Add SetGraphicOverrideRuleGroupOrder command"), [#1176](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1176 "Add RenameGraphicOverrideRuleGroups (ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_ChangeOverrideRuleGroup)"), [#1075](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1075 "Add SetGraphicOverrideRuleOrder: order the rules of a graphic override rule group"), [#1068](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1068 "Add GetGraphicOverrideRulesByName (ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideRuleByName)"), [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetOverrideRuleList` – Retrieves a list of all override rules in the current plan. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Override_GetOverrideRuleList`)</sub> — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_GetVisualOverriddenImage` – Returns visual overridden preview image for an element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GetVisualOverriddenImage`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_MoveOverrideRule` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Moves an override rule between override rule groups. — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_SetActualOverrideCombination` <sub>AC30</sub> – Applies an override combination to the active window. — [#1011](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1011 "Support Archicad 29 and 30 in ModifyOpenings"), [#942](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/942 "Graphic overrides: get and set the current window's override combination, read an element's effective override style")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_SetOverrideRuleGroupOrder` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Sets the order of rule groups in the current project. — [#1178](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1178 "Add SetGraphicOverrideRuleGroupOrder command"), [#1165](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1165 "Expose ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_SetOverrideRuleGroupOrder")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_SetOverrideRuleOrder` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Sets the order of rules in the specified override rule group. — [#1075](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1075 "Add SetGraphicOverrideRuleOrder: order the rules of a graphic override rule group"), [#1064](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1064 "Expose ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_SetOverrideRuleOrder")

## Property

- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_ChangePropertyDefinition` – Changes an already existing property definition in the current plan (identified by its guid).
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_ChangePropertyGroup` – Changes an already existing property group in the current plan (identified by its guid).
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Property_CheckPropertyExpressionString` – Checks if the given property expression is a syntactically valid one. <sub>(was `APIAny_CheckPropertyExpressionStringID`)</sub> — [#1146](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1146 "[1/4] Add CheckPropertyExpressions and GetPropertyExpressionReferences"), [#1145](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1145 "Expose ACAPI_Property missing functions")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_CreatePropertyDefinition` – Creates a property definition in the current plan.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_CreatePropertyGroup` – Creates a property group in the current plan.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_DeletePropertyDefinition` – Deletes a property definition in the current plan.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_DeletePropertyGroup` – Deletes a property group and all of the property definitions inside in the current plan.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyDefinition` – Retrieves one property definition identified by its guid. — [#1149](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1149 "[4/4] Make property definitions available or unavailable for elements (Archicad 27)"), [#1148](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1148 "[3/4] ModifyPropertyValueOfElements: set one property value on several elements, all or nothing (AC27+)"), [#1147](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1147 "[2/4] Add CheckPropertyValues command (ACAPI_Property_IsValidValue)"), [#1146](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1146 "[1/4] Add CheckPropertyExpressions and GetPropertyExpressionReferences"), [#968](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/968 "[1/3] Add Get/SetPropertyValuesOfElementDefaults")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyDefinitions` – Retrieves all the property definitions from a given group.
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyExprReferenceString` – Returns the string that can be used in expressions to refer to a specific property. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetPropertyExprReferenceStringID`)</sub> — [#1146](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1146 "[1/4] Add CheckPropertyExpressions and GetPropertyExpressionReferences"), [#1145](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1145 "Expose ACAPI_Property missing functions")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyGroup` – Retrieves one property group identified by its guid.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyGroups` – Retrieves all the property groups.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyValueString` – Convert an API property value to display string.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_Import` – Imports a property xml.
- ➖ `ACAPI_Property_InstallPropertyObjectHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the property handler function for the given menu item. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Property_InstallPropertyVisibilityHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the property visibility handler function. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Property_IsValidValue` – Returns true if the value is acceptable for the given property definition. — [#1147](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1147 "[2/4] Add CheckPropertyValues command (ACAPI_Property_IsValidValue)"), [#1145](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1145 "Expose ACAPI_Property missing functions")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Property_ModifyPropertyValue` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Sets the value of a property for multiple elements. If the property is not available for at least one of the given element then returns with an error without changing any property value. — [#1148](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1148 "[3/4] ModifyPropertyValueOfElements: set one property value on several elements, all or nothing (AC27+)"), [#1145](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1145 "Expose ACAPI_Property missing functions")
- ➖ `ACAPI_Property_RegisterPropertyObjectHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers a property handler. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Property_RegisterPropertyVisibilityHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Tells Archicad that your add-on wants to modify property visibility. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_SetPropertyValueFromString` – Sets the value of a property value from a passed string. — [#1148](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1148 "[3/4] ModifyPropertyValueOfElements: set one property value on several elements, all or nothing (AC27+)"), [#1147](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1147 "[2/4] Add CheckPropertyValues command (ACAPI_Property_IsValidValue)"), [#968](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/968 "[1/3] Add Get/SetPropertyValuesOfElementDefaults")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Property_AddProperty` <sub>AC27</sub> – Makes a property definition available for all of given elements (it is created if it does not exist). — [#1149](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1149 "[4/4] Make property definitions available or unavailable for elements (Archicad 27)"), [#1145](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1145 "Expose ACAPI_Property missing functions")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Property_DeleteProperty` <sub>AC27</sub> – Makes a property definition unavailable for all of given elements. — [#1149](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1149 "[4/4] Make property definitions available or unavailable for elements (Archicad 27)"), [#1145](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1145 "Expose ACAPI_Property missing functions")

## AddOnObject

- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_CreateClientOnlyObject` – Creates an AddOnObject.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_CreateObject` – Creates an AddOnObject.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_CreateUniqueObject` – Creates a Unique AddOnObject.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_CreateUniqueObjectMore` – Creates more Unique AddOnObjects.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_DeleteObject` – Retrieves an attribute from the database.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_ExistsObject` – Determines if the project database contains the specified AddOnObject or not.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_GetClientOnlyObjectGuidFromName` – Returns the GUID of the specified object.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_GetObjectContent` – Retrieves the content and the name of the specified AddOnObject from the project database.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_GetObjectGuidFromName` – Returns the GUID of the specified object.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_GetObjectList` – Returns the list of the AddOnObjects.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_GetTeamworkOwnerId` – Returns the ownerId of the specified object in teamwork mode.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_GetUniqueObjectGuidFromName` – Returns the GUID of the specified Unique AddOnObject.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_GrantObjects` – In teamwork mode this function grants the specified objects to the given user.
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_InstallAddOnObjectConvertNewFormatHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for converting old-format AddOnObject data into the current format. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_InstallAddOnObjectMergeHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for handling the AddOnObject merge operations. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_InstallAddOnObjectSaveOldFormatHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for converting AddOnObject data into an older format. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_ModifyObject` – Modifies the content and/or the name of the specified AddOnObject.
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_RegisterAddOnObjectHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers the add-on to be called for merging and converting its custom data stored in AddOnObjects. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_ReleaseObjects` – In teamwork mode this function releases the specified objects.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_RequestObjects` – In teamwork mode this function sends a request mail in order to request the specified objects from the owners.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_ReserveObjects` – In teamwork mode this function reserves the specified objects.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_AddOnObject_SetUIProperties` – Sets various UI properties of an AddOnObject.

## LibraryManagement

- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_AddLibraries` – Registers a new folder into the list of active libraries. <sub>(was `APIEnv_AddLibrariesID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_ChangeGDLDate` – Changes GDL environment date (GDL_PROJECT_DATE). <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeGDLDateID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_CheckLibraries` – Checks the currently loaded libraries. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CheckLibrariesID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_CopyFilesIntoLibrary` – Copies files into a folder inside a loaded library. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CopyFilesIntoLibraryID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_CreateCopyOfGdlUserGlobalsHandle` – Returns a copy of the current GDL globals. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CreateCopyOfGdlUserGlobalsHandleID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_CreateFolderInLibrary` – Creates a new folder inside a loaded library. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CreateFolderInLibraryID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_DeleteEmbeddedLibItem` – Deletes the given embedded library item (folder or library part). <sub>(was `APIEnv_DeleteEmbeddedLibItemID`)</sub> — [#957](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/957 "[1/2] Add CreateLibraryParts command: GDL objects from scripts and parameters")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_DeleteEmbeddedLibItems` – Delete embeded lib items. <sub>(was `APIEnv_DeleteEmbeddedLibItemsID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_DestroyCopyOfGdlUserGlobalsHandle` – Destroys the previously copied GDL globals handle. <sub>(was `APIEnv_DestroyCopyOfGdlUserGlobalsHandleID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_GetGDLDate` – Returns GDL environment date information (GDL_PROJECT_DATE). <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetGDLDateID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_GetLibPartOrigLocation` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the original location of a library part. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLibPartOrigLocationID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_GetLibraries` – Returns the list of loaded libraries. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLibrariesID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_InterpretGDLScript3D` – Interprets a 3D GDL script with the same settings as GDL Editor. <sub>(was `APIAny_InterpretGDLScript3DID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_NewLibDial` – Instructs the user to select a new library folder in a dialog. <sub>(was `APIEnv_NewLibDialID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_OpenLibraryPart` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Opens the given library part in the Library Part Editor window. <sub>(was `APIDo_OpenLibraryPartID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_OverwriteLibPart` – Sets the overwrite mode for the library part create function. <sub>(was `APIEnv_OverwriteLibPartID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_ResetLibraries` – Resets the loaded libraries. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ResetLibrariesID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_RunGDLParScript` – Run GDL parameter script. <sub>(was `APIAny_RunGDLParScriptID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_SetLibraries` – Changes the list of loaded libraries. <sub>(was `APIEnv_SetLibrariesID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_UsableGDLString` – Determines whether the string is a GDL keyword or reserved symbol. <sub>(was `APIAny_UsableGDLStringID`)</sub>
