## ProjectOperation

- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_AdditionalFileWasCreated` – Informs Archicad about the additional file created during exporting or publishing. Required for handling them with the target file. (E.g., uploading to BIMcloud together.) <sub>(was `ACAPI_Inform_AdditionalFileWasCreated`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_CatchProjectEvent` – Register or unregister your add-on to be notified of application and project events. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchProjectEvent`)</sub> — [#965](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/965 "[1/2] Add element change tracking: CaptureElementState, GetElementChangesSince, ReleaseElementState") *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Close` – Closes the current project window. <sub>(was `APIDo_CloseID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_GetMiscAppInfo` – Gets miscellaneous information about application. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetMiscAppInfoID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_GetMiscPlotterInfo` – Gets miscellaneous information about plotter. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetMiscPlotterInfoID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_GetMiscPrinterInfo` – Gets miscellaneous information about current printer. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetMiscPrinterInfoID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_GetPrinterPars` – Retrieves the current printer settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetPrinterParsID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_NewProject` – Opens a new plan. <sub>(was `APIDo_NewProjectID`)</sub> — [#952](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/952 "Add the NewProject command"), [#928](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/928 "Add NewProject command")
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Open` – Opens a new project file. <sub>(was `APIDo_OpenID`)</sub> — [#793](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/793 "Add a command to open a Teamwork/BIMcloud project by launching a new Archicad instance"), [#791](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/791 "Add a way to open a Teamwork/BIMcloud project, not just local .pln/.pla files")
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Print` – Prints the content of the current window. <sub>(was `APIDo_PrintID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Project` – Returns information on the current project file. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ProjectID`)</sub> — [#1021](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1021 "[2/3] Add GetReservationStatusOfElements and GrantElements Teamwork commands"), [#955](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/955 "[1/3] Teamwork: add the GetTeamworkProjectInfo command"), [#841](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/841 "DeleteNavigatorItems: report a refused delete as a failure instead of success")
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Publish` – Publishes a publisher set. <sub>(was `APIDo_PublishID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Quit` – Terminates the server application. <sub>(was `APIDo_QuitID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_ReloadLibraries` – Resets and loads the active libraries. <sub>(was `APIDo_ReloadLibrariesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Save` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Saves the content of the current window in different file formats.
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_SaveAsModuleFile` – Saves the selected elements into a module file. <sub>(was `APIDo_SaveAsModuleFileID`)</sub>

## UserInput

- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_CancelUserInput` – Cancels the user input. <sub>(was `APIIo_CancelUserInputID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_ClearElementHighlight` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Removes element highlights from 2D (floor plan and section) and 3D window. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Interface_ClearElementHighlight`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_ClearNeigFilter` – Sets one of the neig types in the neig filter not to be filtered. <sub>(was `APIIo_ClearNeigFilterID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_CompleteUserInput` – Finishes the user input. <sub>(was `APIIo_CompleteUserInputID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_DrawRubberArc` – Draws graphical feedback during an input process. <sub>(was `APIIo_DrawRubberArcID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_DrawRubberLine` – Draws graphical feedback during an input process. <sub>(was `APIIo_DrawRubberLineID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_GetArc` – Performs an arc input. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetArcID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_GetLine` – Performs a line input. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetLineID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_GetMagicWandSets` – Returns information on curve tracing parameters. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetMagicWandSetsID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_GetPoint` – Performs a point input. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetPointID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_GetPoly` – Performs polygon input. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetPolyID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_InitNeigFilter` – Initializes the neig filter for an input. <sub>(was `APIIo_InitNeigFilterID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_ResetConstraint` – Dissolve the drawing constraint. <sub>(was `APIIo_ResetConstraintID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_SetConstraint` – Set the drawing constraint for graphical user input. <sub>(was `APIIo_SetConstraintID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_SetElementHighlight` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Highlight elements in the 2D (floor plan and section) and 3D window. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Interface_SetElementHighlight`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_SetNeigFilter` – Sets one of the neig types in the neig filter to be filtered. <sub>(was `APIIo_SetNeigFilterID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*

## Hotlink

- 🚧 `ACAPI_Hotlink_BreakHotlinkNode` – Breaks the link of the given hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_BreakHotlinkNodeID`)</sub> — [#956](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/956 "Add BreakHotlinkNodes and DeleteHotlinkNodes, report hotlink source status"), [#924](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/924 "Hotlinks: break and delete hotlink nodes, report missing sources")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_CreateHotlinkNode` – Gets the story info from a hotlink source file. <sub>(was `APIDb_CreateHotlinkNodeID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Hotlink_DeleteHotlinkNode` – Deletes the given hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_DeleteHotlinkNodeID`)</sub> — [#956](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/956 "Add BreakHotlinkNodes and DeleteHotlinkNodes, report hotlink source status"), [#924](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/924 "Hotlinks: break and delete hotlink nodes, report missing sources")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetContainingHotlinkGuid` – Retrieves the guid of the hotlink element which contains the given element. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetContainingHotlinkGuidID`)</sub> — [#1110](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1110 "Add GetHotlinkOwnersOfElements (which hotlink an element comes from)"), [#1104](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1104 "Expose ACAPI_Hotlink_GetContainingHotlinkGuid and ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotLinkOwner (which hotlink an element comes from)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotLinkOwner` – Returns the identifier of the hotlink instance the element belongs to (see @ref API_HotlinkType). <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotLinkOwnerID`)</sub> — [#1110](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1110 "Add GetHotlinkOwnersOfElements (which hotlink an element comes from)"), [#1104](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1104 "Expose ACAPI_Hotlink_GetContainingHotlinkGuid and ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotLinkOwner (which hotlink an element comes from)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkInstances` – Returns a list of hotlink instance elements placed from the given hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkInstancesID`)</sub> — [#998](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/998 "GetHotlinks: list the placed instances of each hotlink node"), [#983](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/983 "GetHotlinks: list the placed instances of each hotlink node")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkNode` – Returns a hotlink node identified by its guid. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkNodeID`)</sub> — [#1115](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1115 "Add GetHotlinkDesignOptionTables and SetHotlinkDesignOptionTables"), [#956](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/956 "Add BreakHotlinkNodes and DeleteHotlinkNodes, report hotlink source status")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkNodeTree` – Retrieves the tree structure under a given hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkNodeTreeID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkNodes` – Returns the list of hotlink nodes. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkNodesID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkProxyElementTable` – Returns the mapping table of the proxy elements and the source elements of the given hotlink instance. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkProxyElementTableID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkRootNodeGuid` – Retrieves the Guid of the Hotlink Module type or XRef type hotlink root node. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkRootNodeGuidID`)</sub>
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkSourceStatus` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the status of the given hotlink source file. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkSourceStatusID`)</sub> — [#924](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/924 "Hotlinks: break and delete hotlink nodes, report missing sources")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkStoryInfo` <sub>AC28–30</sub> – Creates a hotlink node without updating its cache content. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkStoryInfoID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_ModifyHotlinkNode` – Modifies the parameters of a hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_ModifyHotlinkNodeID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_UpdateHotlinkCache` – Updates the cache of the given hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_UpdateHotlinkCacheID`)</sub>

## Classification

- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_ChangeClassificationItem` – Modifies a classification item.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_ChangeClassificationSystem` – Modifies a classification system.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_CreateClassificationItem` – Creates a classification item.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_CreateClassificationSystem` – Creates a classification system.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_DeleteClassificationItem` – Deletes a classification item.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_DeleteClassificationSystem` – Deletes a classification system.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationItem` – Retrieves a classification item identified by its guid.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationItemChildren` – Retrieves the children of a classification item.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationItemParent` – Retrieves the parent of a classification item.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationItemSystem` – Retrieves the system of a classification item.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationSystem` – Retrieves a classification system identified by its guid or by its name and edition version.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationSystemRootItems` – Retrieves the root items of a classification system. — [#1052](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1052 "Graphic override criterion: library part element types, new fields and operators (on top of #994)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationSystems` – Retrieves all of the classification systems in the plan. — [#1052](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1052 "Graphic override criterion: library part element types, new fields and operators (on top of #994)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_Import` – Imports a classification xml.

## Window

- 🙋 `ACAPI_Window_AddTextWindowContent` – Adds new lines to the content of a text window. <sub>(was `APIDb_AddTextWindowContentID`)</sub> — [#1187](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1187 "Expose ACAPI_Window_AddTextWindowContent")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Window_ChangeGridSettings` – Changes the grid setting parameters of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_ChangeGridSettingsID`)</sub> — [#935](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/935 "Get and set snap grid and auto-intersect settings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Window_ChangeWindow` – Changes the current (active) window. <sub>(was `APIDo_ChangeWindowID`)</sub> — [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#648](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/648 "Resync the source view's 2D transform before relinking a Drawing")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Window_Check` – Runs the selected checks of the Action Center. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ActionCenter_Check`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Window_CloseWindow` – Closes a window. <sub>(was `APIDb_CloseWindowID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Window_DestroyWindowValidator` – Destroys an already registered validator for the given guid. <sub>(was `APIDb_DestroyWindowValidatorID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Window_GetCurrentWindow` – Returns the identifier of the current (active) window. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetCurrentWindowID`)</sub> — [#1016](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1016 "Add GetCurrentWindowDrawingScale and SetCurrentWindowDrawingScale"), [#648](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/648 "Resync the source view's 2D transform before relinking a Drawing")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Window_GetDatabaseInfo` – Retrieves information of the given database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetDatabaseInfoID`)</sub> — [#1135](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1135 "Add GetDatabases command to list all databases of given types"), [#1107](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1107 "Add GetContainingDatabases command to find an element's database"), [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#825](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/825 "Change and report the master layout in SetLayoutSettings and GetLayoutSettings"), [#756](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/756 "Rename Project Map viewpoints through their element and verify renames")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Window_GetGridSettings` – Returns the grid setting parameters of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetGridSettingsID`)</sub> — [#935](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/935 "Get and set snap grid and auto-intersect settings")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Window_GetOwnWindows` – Returns the ids of the opened windows in an array. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetOwnWindowsID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Window_NewWindow` – Opens a new window. <sub>(was `APIDb_NewWindowID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Window_ResetCurrentDatabase` – Deletes the database elements of the current window and sets up a new, empty environment. <sub>(was `APIDb_ResetCurrentDatabaseID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Window_ResetCurrentWindow` – Deletes the database elements of the current window and sets up a new, empty environment. <sub>(was `APIDb_ResetCurrentWindowID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Window_SetWindowId` – Changes the guid of a previously registered window. Useful when replacing already opened windows by reusing their window object. <sub>(was `APIDb_SetWindowIdID`)</sub>

## Notification

- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchAttributeReplacement` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Register or unregister your add-on to be notified if some attributes are deleted and replaced by other attributes. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchAttributeReplacement`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchElementReservationChange` – Sets notification handler for element reservation changes. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchElementReservationChange`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchLockableReservationChange` – Sets notification handler for reservation changes of lockable objects. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchLockableReservationChange`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchSelectionChange` – Turns the monitoring of selection changes on/off. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchSelectionChange`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchToolChange` – Turns the monitoring of toolbox item changes on/off. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchToolChange`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchViewEvent` – Register or unregister your add-on to be notified of Project Navigator changes. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchViewEvent`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_ClassificationVisibilityChanged` – Sends out an @c APINotify_ClassificationVisibilityChanged project event notification to the other add-ons to state that at least one classification's visibility has changed. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_ClassificationVisibilityChanged`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_GetObservedElements` – Returns the elements observed by the add-on. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GetObservedElements`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_GetParentElement` – Returns the parent of the actual element your addon received notification for. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_GetParentElement`)</sub> — [#763](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/763 "Do not build the parent element in the element observer") *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_GetTranParams` – Returns the transformation parameters of the actual edit operation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_GetTranParams`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_PropertyVisibilityChanged` – Sends out an @c APINotify_PropertyVisibilityChanged project event notification to the other add-ons to state that at least one property's visibility has changed. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_PropertyVisibilityChanged`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_RegisterEventHandler` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Register an event handler in your add-on. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_RegisterEventHandler`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_UnregisterEventHandler` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Unregister an event handler from your add-on. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_UnregisterEventHandler`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*

## ProjectInfo

- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_CreateField` <sub>AC30</sub> – Creates a new custom ProjectInfo field in the specified group. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_CreateGroup` <sub>AC30</sub> – Creates a new ProjectInfo group. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id"), [#1000](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1000 "[1/3] Project Info groups on Archicad 30: get, create, rename, delete and move groups")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_DeleteField` <sub>AC30</sub> – Deletes a custom ProjectInfo field. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_DeleteGroup` <sub>AC30</sub> – Deletes a ProjectInfo group. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id"), [#1000](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1000 "[1/3] Project Info groups on Archicad 30: get, create, rename, delete and move groups")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_GetField` <sub>AC30</sub> – Retrieves the name and value of a ProjectInfo field. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id"), [#1000](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1000 "[1/3] Project Info groups on Archicad 30: get, create, rename, delete and move groups")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_GetFields` <sub>AC30</sub> – Retrieves the GUIDs of all fields in a ProjectInfo group, including subfields. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id"), [#1000](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1000 "[1/3] Project Info groups on Archicad 30: get, create, rename, delete and move groups")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_GetGroupName` <sub>AC30</sub> – Retrieves the name of a ProjectInfo group. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id"), [#1000](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1000 "[1/3] Project Info groups on Archicad 30: get, create, rename, delete and move groups")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_GetGroups` <sub>AC30</sub> – Retrieves the GUIDs of all ProjectInfo groups. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id"), [#1000](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1000 "[1/3] Project Info groups on Archicad 30: get, create, rename, delete and move groups")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_MoveField` <sub>AC30</sub> – Moves a ProjectInfo field to a new position, possibly in a different group. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_MoveGroup` <sub>AC30</sub> – Moves a ProjectInfo group to a new position. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id"), [#1000](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1000 "[1/3] Project Info groups on Archicad 30: get, create, rename, delete and move groups")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_RenameField` <sub>AC30</sub> – Renames a custom ProjectInfo field. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_RenameGroup` <sub>AC30</sub> – Renames a ProjectInfo group. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id"), [#1000](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1000 "[1/3] Project Info groups on Archicad 30: get, create, rename, delete and move groups")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectInfo_SetFieldValue` <sub>AC30</sub> – Sets the value of a ProjectInfo field. — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id")

## AddOnAddOnCommunication

- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_AddMDCLParameter` – Appends a parameter to the parameter list. <sub>(was `APIAny_AddMDCLParameterID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_Call` – Call a command of an other add-on. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Command_Call`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_CallFromEventLoop` – Calls a command of an add-on from Archicad's main event loop. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Command_CallFromEventLoop`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_ChangeMDCLParameter` – Modifies a parameter on the parameter list. <sub>(was `APIAny_ChangeMDCLParameterID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_ExternalCall` – Call a command of an other add-on in another Archicad instance. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Command_ExternalCall`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_FreeMDCLParameterList` – Frees the parameter list handle. <sub>(was `APIAny_FreeMDCLParameterListID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_GetMDCLParameter` – Retrieves a parameter from the parameter list. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetMDCLParameterID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_GetMDCLParameterNum` – Retrieves the number of the parameters on the parameter list. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetMDCLParameterNumID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_InitMDCLParameterList` – Initializes a parameter list to pass to an other add-on. <sub>(was `APIAny_InitMDCLParameterListID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_InstallAddOnCommandHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the Add-On command handler object. — [#781](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/781 "Full Get/Set/Create access to Model View Option combinations, including custom GDL-contributed fields") *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_RegisterSupportedService` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers a command which other add-ons can use. *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_Test` – Test the availability of a command. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Command_Test`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*

## Markup

- ✅ `ACAPI_Markup_AddComment` – Adds a new comment to the given Mark-Up. <sub>(was `ACAPI_MarkUp_AddComment`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Markup_AttachElements` – Attach elements to an existing Mark-Up as corrected or highlighted elements. <sub>(was `ACAPI_MarkUp_AttachElements`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Markup_Create` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Creates new Mark-Up entry.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Markup_Delete` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Deletes an existing Issue.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Markup_DetachElements` – Detach elements from an existing Mark-Up. <sub>(was `ACAPI_MarkUp_DetachElements`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Markup_ExportToBCF` – Exports a BCF file to specified location. <sub>(was `ACAPI_MarkUp_ExportToBCF`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Markup_GetAttachedElements` – Returns the corrected and highlighted elements of the given Mark-Up. <sub>(was `ACAPI_MarkUp_GetAttachedElements`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Markup_GetComments` – Returns the comments attached to the given Mark-Up entry. <sub>(was `ACAPI_MarkUp_GetComments`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Markup_GetList` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the existing Mark-Up entries which the given element is attached to. — [#969](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/969 "Add the ImportMarkupsFromPDF command")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Markup_ImportFromBCF` – Imports a BCF file. <sub>(was `ACAPI_MarkUp_ImportFromBCF`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Markup_ImportFromPDF` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Imports markups from a PDF file. — [#969](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/969 "Add the ImportMarkupsFromPDF command"), [#946](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/946 "Add ImportMarkupsFromPDF command")
- ➖ `ACAPI_Markup_PDFImportOptions` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Opens the Issue Import from PDF file Options dialog where you can choose an option for how the imported Issues should appear in Issue Organizer. *(user interface)*

## Revision

- 🚧 `ACAPI_Revision_ChangeRVMIssue` – Modify an RVM issue. <sub>(was `APIDb_ChangeRVMIssueID`)</sub> — [#973](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/973 "Add ModifyRevisionIssues: change a revision issue's ID, description and custom scheme data"), [#937](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/937 "Add ModifyRevisionIssues: change a revision issue's ID, description and custom data")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMChangeCustomScheme` – Retrieves change custom scheme. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMChangeCustomSchemeID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMChangeFirstIssue` – Retrieves the issue in which the given change is issued at first. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMChangeFirstIssueID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMChanges` – Retrieves all changes. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMChangesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMChangesFromChangeIds` – Retrieves all changes those given by unique change IDs. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMChangesFromChangeIdsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMDocumentRevisionChanges` – Retrieves all changes belong to the given document revision. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMDocumentRevisionChangesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMDocumentRevisions` – Retrieves all document revisions. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMDocumentRevisionsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMElemChangeIds` – Retrieves the change IDs of the given elem identified by GUID. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMElemChangeIdsID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMIssueCustomScheme` – Retrieves issue custom scheme. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMIssueCustomSchemeID`)</sub> — [#973](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/973 "Add ModifyRevisionIssues: change a revision issue's ID, description and custom scheme data"), [#937](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/937 "Add ModifyRevisionIssues: change a revision issue's ID, description and custom data")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMIssueDocumentRevisions` – Retrieves all document revisions belong to the given issue. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMIssueDocumentRevisionsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMIssues` – Retrieves all issues. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMIssuesID`)</sub> — [#973](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/973 "Add ModifyRevisionIssues: change a revision issue's ID, description and custom scheme data")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Revision_GetRVMLayoutCurrentRevisionChanges` – Retrieves all changes belong to the last revision of the given layout. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetRVMLayoutCurrentRevisionChangesID`)</sub>

## AutoText

- ✅ `ACAPI_AutoText_ChangeAutoTextFlag` – Switches autotexts to be replaced with their current values or kept as autotext keys in retrieved texts. <sub>(was `APIAny_ChangeAutoTextFlagID`)</sub> — [#1097](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1097 "ModifyTexts/ModifyLabels: handle autotext keys like CreateTexts"), [#1095](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1095 "Add ChangeTextContent (ACAPI_Element_ChangeContent) and rawText of Text/Label content")
- ✅ `ACAPI_AutoText_CreateAnAutoText` – Creates a new entry in the Project Info. <sub>(was `APIAny_CreateAnAutoTextID`)</sub> — [#674](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/674 "Verify project info field create/delete instead of trusting NoError (#667)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_AutoText_DeleteAnAutoText` – Deletes a custom entry from the Project Info. <sub>(was `APIAny_DeleteAnAutoTextID`)</sub> — [#674](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/674 "Verify project info field create/delete instead of trusting NoError (#667)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_AutoText_GetAutoTextFlag` – Returns the mode flag currently set for autotext replacing in retrieved texts. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetAutoTextFlagID`)</sub> — [#1097](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1097 "ModifyTexts/ModifyLabels: handle autotext keys like CreateTexts"), [#1095](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1095 "Add ChangeTextContent (ACAPI_Element_ChangeContent) and rawText of Text/Label content")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_AutoText_GetAutoTextKeys` – Retrieves all autotext keys and their values. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetAutoTextKeysID`)</sub> — [#1094](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1094 "Add GetAllAutoTextKeys and InterpretAutoTexts commands"), [#1091](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1091 "Expose ACAPI_AutoText_GetAutoTextKeys and ACAPI_AutoText_InterpretAutoText (find and test non-property Autotext keys)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_AutoText_GetAutoTexts` – Retrieves the key, description and value of the currently available autotexts. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetAutoTextsID`)</sub> — [#1012](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1012 "[3/3] Grasshopper components for the Project Info groups and fields of Archicad 30"), [#1010](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1010 "[2/3] Project Info fields on Archicad 30: rename and move fields, create in a group, delete by field id"), [#1000](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1000 "[1/3] Project Info groups on Archicad 30: get, create, rename, delete and move groups"), [#674](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/674 "Verify project info field create/delete instead of trusting NoError (#667)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_AutoText_GetNoteAutotexts` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns a map that contains an element type set for every available autotext regards to the note owner's type. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GetNoteAutotexts`)</sub> — [#1181](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1181 "Add GetNoteAutotexts command (ACAPI_AutoText_GetNoteAutotexts)"), [#1166](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1166 "Expose ACAPI_AutoText_GetNoteAutotexts")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_AutoText_GetNoteKeywordForAutotextID` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the unistring keyword for note autotext id. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GetNoteKeywordForAutotextID`)</sub> — [#1179](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1179 "Add GetNoteAutoTextKeywords (ACAPI_AutoText_GetNoteKeywordForAutotextID)"), [#1168](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1168 "Expose ACAPI_AutoText_GetNoteKeywordForAutotextID")
- ✅ `ACAPI_AutoText_GetPropertyAutoTextKeyTable` – Retrieves the context dependent autotexts keys. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetPropertyAutoTextKeyTableID`)</sub> — [#1091](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1091 "Expose ACAPI_AutoText_GetAutoTextKeys and ACAPI_AutoText_InterpretAutoText (find and test non-property Autotext keys)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_AutoText_InterpretAutoText` – Retrieves the context dependent autotext's value. <sub>(was `APIAny_InterpretAutoTextID`)</sub> — [#1094](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1094 "Add GetAllAutoTextKeys and InterpretAutoTexts commands"), [#1091](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1091 "Expose ACAPI_AutoText_GetAutoTextKeys and ACAPI_AutoText_InterpretAutoText (find and test non-property Autotext keys)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_AutoText_SetAnAutoText` – Sets the value of an autotext. <sub>(was `APIAny_SetAnAutoTextID`)</sub> — [#674](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/674 "Verify project info field create/delete instead of trusting NoError (#667)")

## Drawing

- 🚧 `ACAPI_Drawing_ChangeDrawingScale` – Changes the drawing scale. <sub>(was `APIDb_ChangeDrawingScaleID`)</sub> — [#1016](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1016 "Add GetCurrentWindowDrawingScale and SetCurrentWindowDrawingScale"), [#1015](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1015 "Get and set the drawing scale of the current window")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Drawing_ChangeMarkerDrawingEnabled` – Sets whether attached objects (window/door markers, drawing titles, etc.) should be processed and returned during primitive decomposition. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeMarkerDrawingEnabledID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Drawing_CheckDrawingStatus` – Checks whether the given drawing is up-to-date. <sub>(was `APIDb_CheckDrawingStatusID`)</sub> — [#837](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/837 "Expose Drawing Manager data for Drawings and the renovation filter name of views"), [#836](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/836 "Feature request: Drawing Manager data for Drawing elements")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Drawing_GetDrawingLink` – Returns various information about a drawing link. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetDrawingLinkID`)</sub> — [#837](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/837 "Expose Drawing Manager data for Drawings and the renovation filter name of views"), [#836](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/836 "Feature request: Drawing Manager data for Drawing elements")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Drawing_GetDrawingScale` – Returns the drawing scale of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetDrawingScaleID`)</sub> — [#1016](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1016 "Add GetCurrentWindowDrawingScale and SetCurrentWindowDrawingScale"), [#1015](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1015 "Get and set the drawing scale of the current window")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Drawing_GetFullDrawingContentBox` – Returns the full boundary box of an IDF database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetFullDrawingContentBoxID`)</sub> — [#1131](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1131 "Add GetDrawingContentBoxes (ACAPI_Drawing_GetFullDrawingContentBox)"), [#1122](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1122 "Expose ACAPI_Drawing_GetFullDrawingContentBox (full content box of a placed drawing)")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_Drawing_GetNumberingGridLines` – Retrieves the lines of the grid-based drawing numbering system. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetNumberingGridLinesID`)</sub> — [#1171](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1171 "Expose ACAPI_Drawing_GetNumberingGridLines")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Drawing_IsMarkerDrawingEnabled` – Checks whether attached objects (window/door markers, drawing titles, etc.) are processed and returned during primitive decomposition. <sub>(was `APIEnv_IsMarkerDrawingEnabledID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Drawing_StartDrawingData` – Redirects element creation into a temporary database for creating drawing data. <sub>(was `APIDb_StartDrawingDataID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Drawing_StopDrawingData` – Finalizes the direct creation of drawing elements. <sub>(was `APIDb_StopDrawingDataID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Drawing_Update_Drawings` <sub>AC27–30</sub>

## Grouping

- ✅ `ACAPI_Grouping_ChangeSuspendGroup` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Set 'Suspend Groups' in Archicad. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeSuspendGroupID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Grouping_CreateGroup` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Create an element group.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Grouping_GetAllGroupedElems` – Returns all elements of the given group. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ElementGroup_GetAllGroupedElems`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Grouping_GetConnectedElements` – Returns a list of elements connected to the given element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GetConnectedElements`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Grouping_GetGroup` – Returns the group unique ID of the given element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ElementGroup_GetGroup`)</sub> — [#927](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/927 "Add UngroupElements command and Grasshopper component")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Grouping_GetGroupedElems` – Returns the immediate elements of the given group. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ElementGroup_GetGroupedElems`)</sub> — [#927](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/927 "Add UngroupElements command and Grasshopper component"), [#914](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/914 "Add UngroupElements command")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Grouping_GetMainGroupGuid` – Returns the top level group of a nested group element. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetMainGroupGuidID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Grouping_GetRootGroup` – Returns the top level group unique ID of a nested group element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ElementGroup_GetRootGroup`)</sub> — [#927](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/927 "Add UngroupElements command and Grasshopper component")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Grouping_GetUserData` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Obtains the user data attached to an element group. — [#1065](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1065 "Add GetUserDataOfGroups command (ACAPI_Grouping_GetUserData)"), [#1062](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1062 "Expose ACAPI_Grouping_GetUserData")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Grouping_SetUserData` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Attaches the user data to the element group.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Grouping_Tool` – Executes tool commands on elements. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_Tool`)</sub> — [#927](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/927 "Add UngroupElements command and Grasshopper component"), [#914](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/914 "Add UngroupElements command")

## ProjectSetting

- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_ChangeProjectNotes` – Changes the data of the 'Project Info' dialog. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeProjectNotesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_ChangeStorySettings` – Modifies the story structure. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeStorySettingsID`)</sub> — [#900](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/900 "UpdateZones: optional list of Zones to update instead of the current selection"), [#648](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/648 "Resync the source view's 2D transform before relinking a Drawing")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_GetExportTolerance` – Returns information on curve tracing parameters. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetExportToleranceID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_GetLast3DDefLevels` – Retrieves the 3D default levels for the construction elems. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Database_GetLast3DDefLevels`, `APIDb_GetLast3DDefLevelsID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_GetOffset` – Returns the offset of the origin of the virtual coordinate system. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetOffsetID`)</sub> — [#1123](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1123 "Add GetCoordinateOrigins command (virtual origin offset and user origin)"), [#1117](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1117 "Expose ACAPI_ProjectSetting_GetOffset and ACAPI_Database_GetLocOrigo (virtual origin offset and user origin)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_GetPreferences` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the preferences data. — [#1038](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1038 "[4/4] Add GetCutPolygonsOfElements: the floor plan cut polygons and area of elements"), [#1028](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1028 "[3/4] Add the Zones page to GetProjectPreferences and SetProjectPreferences"), [#1025](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1025 "[2/4] Add the Dimensions page to GetProjectPreferences and SetProjectPreferences"), [#964](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/964 "[1/4] Add GetProjectPreferences and SetProjectPreferences (working units, calculation units, reference levels)")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_GetProjectNotes` – Returns information about the 'Project Info' dialog. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetProjectNotesID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_GetSplineMaxDist` – Returns the maximal distance of a spline segment and a set of coordinates. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetSplineMaxDistID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_GetStorySettings` – Returns the data of the installed stories. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetStorySettingsID`)</sub> — [#900](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/900 "UpdateZones: optional list of Zones to update instead of the current selection"), [#648](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/648 "Resync the source view's 2D transform before relinking a Drawing")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_SetLast3DDefLevels` – Sets the 3D default levels for the passed construction element types. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Database_SetLast3DDefLevels`, `APIDb_SetLast3DDefLevelsID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ProjectSetting_SetPreferences` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Adjusts the preferences. — [#1028](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1028 "[3/4] Add the Zones page to GetProjectPreferences and SetProjectPreferences"), [#1025](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1025 "[2/4] Add the Dimensions page to GetProjectPreferences and SetProjectPreferences"), [#964](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/964 "[1/4] Add GetProjectPreferences and SetProjectPreferences (working units, calculation units, reference levels)")

## Dialog

- ➖ `ACAPI_Dialog_ActivateSessionReport` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Activates the session report window. <sub>(was `APIAny_ActivateSessionReportID`)</sub> *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Dialog_CreateAttributePicker` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Enables the attribute picker functionality for a push check, if the user clicks the push check, the picker will open. Attributes pickers can be used to choose an attribute. On these new pickers, the folder structure and searching functionality is enabled. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Interface_CreateAttributePicker`)</sub> *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Dialog_ObjectSettings` – Opens the Object/Lamp(/Window/Door) Settings dialog. <sub>(was `APIIo_ObjectSettingsID`)</sub> *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Dialog_OpenLibPartFileDialog` – Invokes the "Open Library Part" dialog. <sub>(was `APIIo_OpenLibPartFileDialogID`)</sub> *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Dialog_OpenOtherObjectDialog` – Invokes the "Open Library" dialog. <sub>(was `APIIo_OpenOtherObjectDialogID`)</sub> *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Dialog_OpenPictureDialog` – Opens an "Open Picture" dialog (same dialog as on Material Settings, Texture tabpage). <sub>(was `APIIo_OpenPictureDialogID`)</sub> *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Dialog_PetPalette` – Opens a pet palette for performing user input from the add-on. <sub>(was `APIIo_PetPaletteID`)</sub> *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Dialog_SaveLibPartFileDialog` – Opens the Save Library Part dialog and returns the selected location. <sub>(was `APIIo_SaveLibPartFileDialogID`)</sub> *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Dialog_SetUserControlCallback` – Helper function to set the proper attribute callback for a user control. <sub>(was `APIIo_SetUserControlCallbackID`)</sub> *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Dialog_SettingsDialog` – Opens a tool settings dialog. <sub>(was `APIIo_SettingsDialogID`)</sub> *(user interface)*

## Favorite

- ✅ `ACAPI_Favorite_Change` – Changes an existing Favorite (identifed by name). — [#813](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/813 "Keep 2D GDL arrays intact on flat-list writes, apply Favorites to windows and doors, capture opening markers")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Favorite_Create` – Creates a new Favorites entry based on the given element. — [#813](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/813 "Keep 2D GDL arrays intact on flat-list writes, apply Favorites to windows and doors, capture opening markers")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Favorite_Delete` – Deletes a Favorites entry.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Favorite_Export` – Exports the Favorites in the plan into a Favorites file or folder.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Favorite_Get` – Loads the parameters of the given favorite. — [#652](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/652 "Drawing favorite fails every time")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Favorite_GetNum` – Returns the names and indexes of the contents of the Favorites list, along with the amount of entries, for a given type and variation.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Favorite_GetPreviewImage` – Returns the favorite preview image.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Favorite_Import` – Imports the contents of a Favorites file or folder into the plan.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Favorite_Read` – Reads the contents of a Favorites file or folder.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Favorite_Rename` – Renames an existing Favorite (identifed by name).

## HierarchicalEditing

- ⬜ `ACAPI_HierarchicalEditing_CancelHierarchicalElem` – Cancel changes in Hierarchical Elem (Curtain Wall, Stair, Railing, Beam, Column) editing mode. <sub>(was `APIDo_CancelHierarchicalElemID`, `APIIo_CancelHierarchicalElemID`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_HierarchicalEditing_EditHierarchicalElem` <sub>(was `APIDo_EditHierarchicalElemID`, `APIIo_EditHierarchicalElemID`)</sub>

Curtain Wall, Beam, Column, Stair, Railing, MEP Elements, etc... — [#1213](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1213 "Add GetEditModeState command (hierarchical editing mode query)"), [#1208](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1208 "Expose ACAPI_HierarchicalEditing_IsIn*EditMode (tell scripts why writes are refused)")

## Licensing

- ➖ `ACAPI_Licensing_CatchLicenseBorrowStatusChange` – Catches notifications on license borrowing status changes. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchLicenseBorrowStatusChange`)</sub> *(add-on licensing)*

- ➖ `ACAPI_Licensing_GetConfigurationNumber` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Retrieves the configuration number of the current license. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Protection_GetConfigurationNumber`)</sub> *(add-on licensing)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Licensing_GetExpirationTime` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Retrieves the expiration time of the current license (if any). <sub>(was `ACAPI_Protection_GetExpirationTime`)</sub> *(add-on licensing)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Licensing_GetNumberOfLicenses` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Retrieves the number of current licenses. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Protection_GetNumberOfLicenses`)</sub> *(add-on licensing)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Licensing_GetPartnerId` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – When Archicad runs on an SSA license, this string contains the internal identifier of the reseller. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Protection_GetPartnerId`)</sub> *(add-on licensing)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Licensing_GetProtectionMode` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Retrieves information about the protection mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Protection_GetProtectionMode`)</sub> *(add-on licensing)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Licensing_GetSerialNumber` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Retrieves the unique serial number of the current license. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Protection_GetSerialNumber`)</sub> *(add-on licensing)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Licensing_InvokeForwardDialog` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Invoke generic SSA Forward dialog that warns that the user doesn't have SSA license. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Interface_InvokeForwardDialog`)</sub> *(add-on licensing)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Licensing_IsSSALevelAtLeast` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns whether the SSA subscription passed in the parameter is available. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Protection_IsSSALevelAtLeast`)</sub> *(add-on licensing)*

## ProcessWindow

- ➖ `ACAPI_ProcessWindow_CloseProcessWindow` – Closes the process window. <sub>(was `APIIo_CloseProcessWindowID`)</sub> *(progress window of the add-on)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProcessWindow_GetProcessControl` – Returns the actual process control from Archicad. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetProcessControlID`)</sub> *(progress window of the add-on)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProcessWindow_GetProcessOnOffSwitch` – Returns whether the process window will appear. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetProcessOnOffSwitchID`)</sub> *(progress window of the add-on)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProcessWindow_IncProcessValue` – Increments the value of the process bar in the process window. <sub>(was `APIIo_IncProcessValueID`)</sub> *(progress window of the add-on)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProcessWindow_InitProcessWindow` – Opens the process window. <sub>(was `APIIo_InitProcessWindowID`)</sub> *(progress window of the add-on)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProcessWindow_IsProcessCanceled` – Checks if the process was canceled by the user. <sub>(was `APIIo_IsProcessCanceledID`)</sub> *(progress window of the add-on)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProcessWindow_SetInnerProcessControl` – Sets the given process control to Archicad. <sub>(was `APIIo_SetInnerProcessControlID`)</sub> *(progress window of the add-on)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProcessWindow_SetNextProcessPhase` – Starts the next process phase in the process window. <sub>(was `APIIo_SetNextProcessPhaseID`)</sub> *(progress window of the add-on)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProcessWindow_SetProcessOnOffSwitch` – Turns the process window on/off. <sub>(was `APIIo_SetProcessOnOffSwitchID`)</sub> *(progress window of the add-on)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProcessWindow_SetProcessValue` – Sets the value of the process bar in the process window. <sub>(was `APIIo_SetProcessValueID`)</sub> *(progress window of the add-on)*

## ModelAccess

- ⬜ `ACAPI_ModelAccess_DecomposePgon` – Decomposes a polygon of the 3D model into convex polygons. <sub>(was `ACAPI_3D_DecomposePgon`)</sub>

- ⬜ `ACAPI_ModelAccess_GetTextureCoord` – Returns the texture coordinates of the given surface point. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetTextureCoordID`)</sub>

## ElementLink

- 🚧 `ACAPI_ElementLink_GetLinkFlags` – Retrieves the link flags associated with the link between the two elements. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GetLinkFlags`)</sub> — [#958](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/958 "[1/2] Add LinkElements, UnlinkElements and GetLinksOfElements commands")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ElementLink_GetLinkedPropertyObjects` – Retrieves property objects linked to an element or the element default. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GetLinkedPropertyObjects`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ElementLink_GetLinks` – Returns a list of elements linked to the given element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GetLinks`)</sub> — [#958](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/958 "[1/2] Add LinkElements, UnlinkElements and GetLinksOfElements commands")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ElementLink_Link` – Links one element to another. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_Link`)</sub> — [#958](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/958 "[1/2] Add LinkElements, UnlinkElements and GetLinksOfElements commands")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_ElementLink_SetLinkedPropertyObjects` – Link property objects to an element or the element default. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_SetLinkedPropertyObjects`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ElementLink_Unlink` – Removes the link from the second element to the first. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_Unlink`)</sub> — [#958](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/958 "[1/2] Add LinkElements, UnlinkElements and GetLinksOfElements commands")

## OldListing

- ➖ `ACAPI_OldListing_Get` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Retrieves the data of a listdata. *(deprecated listing API)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_OldListing_GetLocal` – Retrieves the data of a local listing component. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ListData_GetLocal`)</sub> *(deprecated listing API)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_OldListing_GetNum` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the number of listdata in given set. *(deprecated listing API)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_OldListing_GetSet` – Retrieves the data of a listdata set. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ListData_GetSet`)</sub> *(deprecated listing API)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_OldListing_GetSetNum` – Returns the number of listdata sets. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ListData_GetSetNum`)</sub> *(deprecated listing API)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_OldListing_Search` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Searches for a listdata. *(deprecated listing API)*

## Selection

- 🚧 `ACAPI_Selection_DeselectAll` – Adds/removes a number of elements to/from the current selection. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_DeselectAll`)</sub> — [#963](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/963 "[1/2] Add the DeselectAll, GetMarquee and SetMarquee commands"), [#954](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/954 "Add Get3DStyles, SetCurrent3DStyle and ShowElementsIn3D commands"), [#949](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/949 "Add HideElements and ShowAllHiddenElements commands")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Selection_Get` – Returns information on the selection, and the selected elements. — [#1001](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1001 "Add ZoomToElements to zoom the current 2D or 3D window to elements"), [#963](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/963 "[1/2] Add the DeselectAll, GetMarquee and SetMarquee commands"), [#949](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/949 "Add HideElements and ShowAllHiddenElements commands"), [#900](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/900 "UpdateZones: optional list of Zones to update instead of the current selection")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Selection_GetSelectedElement` – Converts an @ref API_Neig structure to an @ref API_Guid. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetSelectedElementID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Selection_Select` – Adds/removes a number of elements to/from the current selection. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_Select`)</sub> — [#1001](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1001 "Add ZoomToElements to zoom the current 2D or 3D window to elements"), [#954](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/954 "Add Get3DStyles, SetCurrent3DStyle and ShowElementsIn3D commands"), [#949](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/949 "Add HideElements and ShowAllHiddenElements commands"), [#900](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/900 "UpdateZones: optional list of Zones to update instead of the current selection"), [#314](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/314 "Bug: ChangeSelectionOfElements is really slow")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Selection_SetMarquee` – Changes the current marquee selection. — [#963](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/963 "[1/2] Add the DeselectAll, GetMarquee and SetMarquee commands")
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Selection_SetSelectedElementNeig` – Converts an @ref API_Guid structure to an @ref API_Neig. <sub>(was `APIAny_SetSelectedElementNeigID`)</sub>

## Sight

- ⬜ `ACAPI_Sight_CreateSight` – Creates a new sight for 3D operations. <sub>(was `ACAPI_3D_CreateSight`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Sight_DeleteSight` – Deletes the supplied sight. <sub>(was `ACAPI_3D_DeleteSight`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Sight_GetCurrentWindowSight` – Returns the current window sight. <sub>(was `ACAPI_3D_GetCurrentWindowSight`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Sight_GetSelectedSightModel` <sub>AC28–30</sub> – Returns the model of the selected sight.
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Sight_SelectSight` – Switches to the supplied 3D sight for subsequent operations. <sub>(was `ACAPI_3D_SelectSight`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Sight_Switch3DSight` – Switches between different 3D databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_Switch3DSightID`)</sub>

## ModulData

- 🚧 `ACAPI_ModulData_Delete` – Removes the custom data section stored in the project database. — [#1035](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1035 "[2/3] Store custom script data on elements: SetElementUserData, GetElementUserData"), [#961](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/961 "[1/3] Store custom script data in the project: SetProjectData, GetProjectData, DeleteProjectData")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ModulData_Get` – Returns the data section stored in the project database. — [#1035](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1035 "[2/3] Store custom script data on elements: SetElementUserData, GetElementUserData"), [#961](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/961 "[1/3] Store custom script data in the project: SetProjectData, GetProjectData, DeleteProjectData")
- 🙋 `ACAPI_ModulData_GetInfo` – Returns information on the custom data section stored in the project database. — [#1077](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1077 "Expose ACAPI_ModulData_GetInfo")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ModulData_GetList` – Returns a list of names of the custom data sections stored in the project database. — [#1035](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1035 "[2/3] Store custom script data on elements: SetElementUserData, GetElementUserData"), [#961](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/961 "[1/3] Store custom script data in the project: SetProjectData, GetProjectData, DeleteProjectData")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_ModulData_Store` – Saves a custom data section into the project database. — [#1035](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1035 "[2/3] Store custom script data on elements: SetElementUserData, GetElementUserData"), [#961](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/961 "[1/3] Store custom script data in the project: SetProjectData, GetProjectData, DeleteProjectData")

## Rendering

- 🚧 `ACAPI_Rendering_ChangeRenderingSets` – Changes the photorendering settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeRenderingSetsID`)</sub> — [#1004](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1004 "Let PhotoRender set the image size and resolution, report the current rendering scene"), [#960](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/960 "Rendering: set the image size and resolution, report the current scene")

- 🚧 `ACAPI_Rendering_GetRenderingSets` – Returns information on the photorendering settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetRenderingSetsID`)</sub> — [#1004](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1004 "Let PhotoRender set the image size and resolution, report the current rendering scene"), [#960](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/960 "Rendering: set the image size and resolution, report the current scene")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Rendering_PhotoRender` – Creates a photorealistic image of the 3D window. <sub>(was `APIDo_PhotoRenderID`)</sub> — [#1004](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1004 "Let PhotoRender set the image size and resolution, report the current rendering scene"), [#943](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/943 "[1/2] Add the GetRenderingScenes, SetCurrentRenderingScene and PhotoRender commands")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_Rendering_SetCurrentRenderingScene` – Sets the current Rendering Scene to the given named Rendering Scene. <sub>(was `APIEnv_SetCurrentRenderingSceneID`)</sub> — [#1004](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1004 "Let PhotoRender set the image size and resolution, report the current rendering scene"), [#943](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/943 "[1/2] Add the GetRenderingScenes, SetCurrentRenderingScene and PhotoRender commands")

## SurveyPoint

- 🚧 `ACAPI_SurveyPoint_GetSurveyPointTransformation` – Returns the transformation matrix that transforms points given in the Project Origin's coordinate system into the Survey Point's coordinate system. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetSurveyPointTransformationID`)</sub> — [#1215](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1215 "Add GetSurveyPointTransformation command"), [#1212](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1212 "Expose ACAPI_SurveyPoint_GetSurveyPointTransformation (project ↔ survey coordinates)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_SurveyPoint_IsSurveyPointLocked` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the lock state of the Survey Point. <sub>(was `APIEnv_IsSurveyPointLockedID`)</sub> — [#970](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/970 "Add survey point visibility and lock to GetGeoLocation and SetGeoLocation"), [#938](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/938 "Get/SetGeoLocation: survey point visibility and lock")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_SurveyPoint_IsSurveyPointVisible` – Checks if the survey point is visible. <sub>(was `APIEnv_IsSurveyPointVisibleID`)</sub> — [#970](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/970 "Add survey point visibility and lock to GetGeoLocation and SetGeoLocation"), [#938](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/938 "Get/SetGeoLocation: survey point visibility and lock")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_SurveyPoint_SetSurveyPointLocked` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Locks or Unlocks the Survey Point. <sub>(was `APIEnv_SetSurveyPointLockedID`)</sub> — [#970](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/970 "Add survey point visibility and lock to GetGeoLocation and SetGeoLocation"), [#938](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/938 "Get/SetGeoLocation: survey point visibility and lock")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_SurveyPoint_SetSurveyPointVisibility` – Sets the visibility of the survey point. <sub>(was `APIEnv_SetSurveyPointVisibilityID`)</sub> — [#970](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/970 "Add survey point visibility and lock to GetGeoLocation and SetGeoLocation"), [#938](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/938 "Get/SetGeoLocation: survey point visibility and lock")

## Command

- ➖ `ACAPI_Command_ExecuteJSONRequest` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Executes an Archicad JSON interface command. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Command_GetHttpConnectionPort` – Returns the HTTP port number, on which Archicad is expecting requests. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetHttpConnectionPortID`)</sub> *(add-on infrastructure)*

## DifferenceGenerator

- 🚧 `ACAPI_DifferenceGenerator_GenerateDifference` – It gives back the differences between two states. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ElemDifferenceGenerator_GenerateDifference`)</sub> — [#965](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/965 "[1/2] Add element change tracking: CaptureElementState, GetElementChangesSince, ReleaseElementState")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_DifferenceGenerator_GetState` – Classifies an element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ElemDifferenceGenerator_GetState`)</sub> — [#965](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/965 "[1/2] Add element change tracking: CaptureElementState, GetElementChangesSince, ReleaseElementState")

## 3D

- 🙋 `ACAPI_3D_GetNum` <sub>AC25–26</sub> — [#1067](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1067 "Expose ACAPI_3D_GetNum")

## AddOnIdentification

- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIdentification_Application` – Returns information about the current application. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ApplicationID`)</sub> *(add-on infrastructure)*

## Override

- 🙋 `ACAPI_Override_GetOverrideRule` <sub>AC25–26</sub> — [#1059](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1059 "Expose ACAPI_Override_GetOverrideRule")

## ProjectSettings

- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectSettings_GetSpecFolder` – Returns the location of special folders. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetSpecFolderID`)</sub> — [#957](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/957 "[1/2] Add CreateLibraryParts command: GDL objects from scripts and parameters")

- ⬜ `ACAPI_SetPreferences_OldVersion` <sub>AC25–26</sub>
